Dey wa’n’t ’lowed ter sing, ner dance, ner play de banjo w’en Mars Jeems wuz roun’ de place; fer Mars Jeems say he wouldn’ hab no sech gwines-on﻿—said he bought his han’s ter wuk, en not ter play, en w’en night come dey mus’ sleep en res’, so dey’d be ready ter git up soon in de mawnin’ en go ter dey wuk fresh en strong.

===Chinese===
“詹姆斯·麦克莱恩老爷（Mars Jeems McLean），”朱利叶斯说，“就是刚打我们跟前过、抽他马儿的那位绅士的爷爷。他有老大一片种植园，一大帮黑奴。詹姆斯老爷是个狠心人，对手下的工人苛得没边儿。打从长大成人起，他就没见对谁有过一点情分。他爹老约翰·麦克莱恩老爷（John McLean）一死，种植园连同所有的黑奴就都落到小詹姆斯老爷手里。他先前就够坏的了，可没过多久，就闹到这步田地——要是得在詹姆斯老爷手底下过日子，那活着简直一点意思都没有。他家的黑奴得天蒙蒙亮干到天擦黑，别人家的奴隶顶多日出而作、日落而息；他们到嘴的吃食也就勉勉强强，还尽是最粗劣的伙食。詹姆斯老爷在庄子上一转，就不许人唱歌、跳舞、弹班卓琴；因为詹姆斯老爷说他容不得这路胡闹——说他买这些工人是来做工的，不是来耍的，天一黑就得睡觉歇乏，好赶明儿一早麻利起来，精精神神、结结实实地上工去。

===Original===
“Mars Jeems didn’ ’low no co’tin’ er juneseyin’ roun’ his plantation﻿—said he wanted his niggers ter put dey min’s on dey wuk, en not be wastin’ dey time wid no sech foolis’ness. En he wouldn’ let his han’s git married﻿—said he wuzn’ raisin’ niggers, but wuz raisin’ cotton. En w’eneber any er de boys en gals ’ud ’mence ter git sweet on one ernudder, he’d sell one er de yuther un ’em, er sen’ ’em way down in Robeson County ter his yuther plantation, whar dey couldn’ nebber see one ernudder.

===Chinese===
“詹姆斯老爷不许庄子上有人谈情说爱、缠什么心上人（junesey）——说他要黑奴们把心思都放在活计上，别把工夫糟蹋在这种蠢事上。他也不让手下的工人成亲——说他养的不是黑奴，是棉花。每逢哪个小伙子跟大姑娘要好起来，他就把其中一个卖掉，要么打发到远在罗伯逊县（Robeson County）他另一处种植园去，叫他们这辈子再也见不上一面。

===Original===
“Ef any er de niggers eber complained, dey got fo’ty; so co’se dey didn’ many un ’em complain. But dey didn’ lack it, des de same, en nobody couldn’ blame ’em, fer dey had a ha’d time. Mars Jeems didn’ make no ’lowance fer nachul bawn laz’ness, ner sickness, ner trouble in de min’, ner nuffin; he wuz des gwine ter git so much wuk outer eve’y han’, er know de reason w’y.

===Chinese===
“哪个黑奴敢抱怨，就换四十鞭；自然没几个人抱怨了。可他们心里照样不痛快，这也怪不得他们，日子实在太苦。詹姆斯老爷一点不通融——天生的懒散也好，闹病也好，心里存了事也好，一概不认；他就是要从每个人身上榨出那么些活来，短一分都得说出个道道来。

===Original===
“Dey wuz one time de niggers ’lowed, fer a spell, dat Mars Jeems mought git bettah. He tuk a lackin’ ter Mars Marrabo McSwayne’s oldes’ gal, Miss Libbie, en useter go ober dere eve’y day er eve’y ebenin’, en folks said dey wuz gwine ter git married sho’. But it ’pears dat Miss Libbie heared ’bout de gwines-on on Mars Jeems’s plantation, en she des ’lowed she couldn’ trus’ herse’f wid no sech a man; dat he mought git so useter ’busin’ his niggers dat he’d ’mence ter ’buse his wife atter he got useter habbin’ her roun’ de house. So she ’clared she wuzn’ gwine ter hab nuffin mo’ ter do wid young Mars Jeems.

===Chinese===
“有一阵子，黑奴们还当詹姆斯老爷兴许会变好呢。他看上了马拉波·麦克斯韦恩老爷（Marrabo McSwayne）家的大小姐利比小姐（Libbie），天天或者每天傍晚往那边跑，人家都说这门亲事准成了。可利比小姐不知怎么听说了詹姆斯老爷庄子上的那些名堂，就认定这么个男人托付不得；想着他打骂黑奴惯了，等家里娶惯了她，兴许回头也要打骂起老婆来。于是她放话，再也不跟小詹姆斯老爷相干了。

===Original===
“De niggers wuz all monst’us sorry w’en de match wuz bust’ up, fer now Mars Jeems got wusser ’n he wuz befo’ he sta’ted sweethea’tin’. De time he useter spen’ co’tin’ Miss Libbie he put in findin’ fault wid de niggers, en all his bad feelin’s ’ca’se Miss Libbie th’owed ’im ober he ’peared ter try ter wuk off on de po’ niggers.

===Chinese===
“这门亲事一散伙，黑奴们个个懊丧得不行，因为詹姆斯老爷比起意追求人之前变得更凶了。从前他用来向利比小姐献殷勤的那些工夫，如今都拿去挑黑奴的错；利比小姐把他甩了，他一肚子的邪火，仿佛都要设法撒到可怜的黑奴头上。

===Original===
“W’iles Mars Jeems wuz co’tin’ Miss Libbie, two er de han’s on de plantation had got ter settin’ a heap er sto’ by one ernudder. One un ’em wuz name’ Solomon, en de yuther wuz a ’oman w’at wukked in de fiel’ ’long er ’im﻿—I fe’git dat ’oman’s name, but it doan ’mount ter much in de tale nohow. Now, whuther ’ca’se Mars Jeems wuz so tuk up wid his own junesey dat he didn’ paid no ’tention fer a w’ile ter w’at wuz gwine on ’twix’ Solomon en his junesey, er whuther his own co’tin’ made ’im kin’ er easy on de co’tin’ in de qua’ters, dey ain’ no tellin’. But dey’s one thing sho’, dat w’en Miss Libbie th’owed ’im ober, he foun’ out ’bout Solomon en de gal monst’us quick, en gun Solomon fo’ty, en sont de gal down ter de Robeson County plantation, en tol’ all de niggers ef he ketch ’em at any mo’ sech foolishness, he wuz gwine ter skin ’em alibe en tan dey hides befo’ dey ve’y eyes. Co’se he wouldn’ ’a’ done it, but he mought ’a’ made things wusser ’n dey wuz. So you kin ’magine dey wa’n’t much lub-makin’ in de qua’ters fer a long time.

===Chinese===
“詹姆斯老爷向利比小姐求爱那阵子，庄上有两个工人彼此瞧对了眼，好得跟什么似的。一个名叫所罗门（Solomon），另一个是跟他一块儿在田里做工的女人——那女人叫啥俺忘了，不过在这故事里横竖不要紧。至于是因为詹姆斯老爷一心扑在自己的心上人身上，好一阵子没留意所罗门跟他相好的这档子事，还是他自己谈着恋爱，对黑奴住区里的谈情说爱也就手松了些，那就说不清了。可有一件事是板上钉钉的：利比小姐把他一甩，他立马就发觉了所罗门和那姑娘的事，赏了所罗门四十鞭，把那姑娘打发到罗伯逊县的种植园去，还警告众黑奴，再叫他撞见这路蠢事，就要活剥了他们的皮，当面把皮子鞣成革。他自然不至于真下得去手，可事情真要闹起来，只会比先前更糟。所以你们想吧，往后好长一阵子，黑奴住区里再没什么谈情说爱的光景。

===Original===
“Mars Jeems useter go down ter de yuther plantation sometimes fer a week er mo’, en so he had ter hab a oberseah ter look atter his wuk w’iles he ’uz gone. Mars Jeems’s oberseah wuz a po’ w’ite man name’ Nick Johnson﻿—de niggers called ’im Mars Johnson ter his face, but behin’ his back dey useter call ’im Ole Nick, en de name suited ’im ter a T. He wuz wusser ’n Mars Jeems ever da’ed ter be. Co’se de darkies didn’ lack de way Mars Jeems used ’em, but he wuz de marster, en had a right ter do ez he please’; but dis yer Ole Nick wa’n’t nuffin but a po’ buckrah, en all de niggers ’spised ’im ez much ez dey hated ’im, fer he didn’ own nobody, en wa’n’t no bettah ’n a nigger, fer in dem days any ’spectable pusson would ruther be a nigger dan a po’ w’ite man.

===Chinese===
“詹姆斯老爷有时要下那处种植园去待上一个礼拜还多，所以得雇个监工，替他照管这边的活计。詹姆斯老爷的监工是个穷白人，名叫尼克·约翰逊（Nick Johnson）——黑奴们当面叫他约翰逊老爷，背地里都管他叫老尼克，这名字搁他身上再合适不过。他比詹姆斯老爷敢做的还狠。黑奴们自然不喜欢詹姆斯老爷那套使唤人的法子，可他到底是主子，爱怎么便怎么；这位老尼克却不过是个穷白人（buckrah），黑奴们既恨他，又一样地看不起他，因为他名下一个人也没有，并不比黑奴强到哪儿去——那年头，但凡要些脸面的人，宁可做黑奴，也不愿做穷白人。

===Original===
“Now, atter Solomon’s gal had be’n sont away, he kep’ feelin’ mo’ en mo’ bad erbout it, ’tel fin’lly he ’lowed he wuz gwine ter see ef dey couldn’ be sump’n done fer ter git ’er back, en ter make Mars Jeems treat de darkies bettah. So he tuk a peck er co’n out’n de ba’n one night, en went ober ter see ole Aun’ Peggy, de free-nigger cunjuh ’oman down by de Wim’l’ton Road.

===Chinese===
“且说所罗门的心上人被送走之后，他心里越来越不是滋味，到后来便打定主意，要去看看有没有法子把她弄回来，也叫詹姆斯老爷善待黑奴。于是有一天夜里，他从谷仓里舀了一配克玉米，去拜访住在威明顿大道（Wim'l'ton Road）边上的那个自由黑人巫女——佩姬大娘。

===Original===
“Aun’ Peggy listen’ ter ’is tale, en ax’ him some queshtuns, en den tol’ ’im she’d wuk her roots, en see w’at dey’d say ’bout it, en ter-morrer night he sh’d come back ag’in en fetch ernudder peck er co’n, en den she’d hab sump’n fer ter tell ’im.

===Chinese===
“佩姬大娘听他讲了来龙去脉，问了他几句，然后说她要施一施她的法，看看卦上怎么说，明儿夜里让他再跑一趟，再捎一配克玉米来，到那时她就有话交代他了。

===Original===
“So Solomon went back de nex’ night, en sho’ ’nuff, Aun’ Peggy tol’ ’im w’at ter do. She gun ’im some stuff w’at look’ lack it be’n made by poundin’ up some roots en yarbs wid a pestle in a mo’tar.

“ ‘Dis yer stuff,’ sez she, ‘is monst’us pow’ful kin’ er goopher. You take dis home, en gin it ter de cook, ef you kin trus’ her, en tell her fer ter put it in yo’ marster’s soup de fus’ cloudy day he hab okra soup fer dinnah. Min’ you follers de d’rections.’

===Chinese===
“第二天夜里所罗门又去了，果不其然，佩姬大娘告诉他该怎么办。她给了他一些东西，看那模样，像是把草根香草搁在臼里，拿杵捣出来的。

“‘这东西，’她说，‘是顶顶厉害的一路咒（goopher）。你拿回家去，交给厨娘——要是她靠得住的话——告诉她，等头一个他午饭喝秋葵汤的阴天，把它下进汤里。记住，照吩咐办事，一步也别走差。’

===Original===
“ ‘It ain’ gwineter p’isen ’im, is it?’ ax’ Solomon, gittin’ kin’ er skeered; fer Solomon wuz a good man, en didn’ want ter do nobody no rale ha’m.

“ ‘Oh, no,’ sez ole Aun’ Peggy, ‘it’s gwine ter do ’im good, but he’ll hab a monst’us bad dream fus’. A mont’ fum now you come down heah en lemme know how de goopher is wukkin’. Fer I ain’ done much er dis kin’ er cunj’in’ er late yeahs, en I has ter kinder keep track un it ter see dat it doan ’complish no mo’ d’n I ’lows fer it ter do. En I has ter be kinder keerful ’bout cunj’in’ w’ite folks; so be sho’ en lemme know, w’ateber you do, des w’at is gwine on roun’ de plantation.’

===Chinese===
“‘这不会把他毒着吧？’所罗门问，心里有点发毛；所罗门是个善人，不想当真害了谁。

“‘哦，不会，’老佩姬大娘说，‘这东西是给他好处，只是他先要做个顶顶凶的噩梦。一个月后的今天，你下来一趟，跟我说说那咒行得怎么样。这些年我不大做这一路法术了，得留心记着它的去向，别叫它办出超出我许可的动静来。对白人施法，我也得格外当心；所以不管怎样，庄上有什么动静，你可千万记得来报我。’

===Original===
“So Solomon say all right, en tuk de goopher mixtry up ter de big house en gun it ter de cook, en tol’ her fer ter put it in Mars Jeems’s soup de fus’ cloudy day she hab okra soup fer dinnah. It happen’ dat de ve’y nex’ day wuz a cloudy day, en so de cook made okra soup fer Mars Jeems’s dinnah, en put de powder Solomon gun her inter de soup, en made de soup rale good, so Mars Jeems eat a whole lot of it en ’peared ter enjoy it.

===Chinese===
“所罗门应承下来，把那咒药带上大宅，交给厨娘，嘱咐她等头一个阴天老爷午饭喝秋葵汤时下进去。碰巧第二天就是个阴天，厨娘便给詹姆斯老爷的午饭做了秋葵汤，把所罗门给她的药粉下进汤里，那汤做得实在地道，詹姆斯老爷喝了一大通，瞧那样子还喝得挺受用。

===Original===
“De nex’ mawnin’ Mars Jeems tol’ de oberseah he wuz gwine ’way on some bizness, en den he wuz gwine ter his yuther plantation, down in Robeson County, en he didn’ ’spec’ he’d be back fer a mont’ er so.

“ ‘But,’ sezee, ‘I wants you ter run dis yer plantation fer all it’s wuth. Dese yer niggers is gittin’ monst’us triflin’ en lazy en keerless, en dey ain’ no ’pen’ence ter be put in ’em. I wants dat stop’, en w’iles I’m gone erway I wants de ’spenses cut ’way down en a heap mo’ wuk done. Fac’, I wants dis yer plantation ter make a reco’d dat’ll show w’at kinder oberseah you is.’

===Chinese===
“第二天一早，詹姆斯老爷告诉监工，说他要出门办点事，然后下罗伯逊县那处种植园去，估摸着一个月上下才能回来。

“‘不过，’他说，‘我要你把这庄子往十足里经营。这些黑奴越来越吊儿郎当、又懒又马虎，指望不上。我要把这股风气刹住，我不在家的时候，开销要大砍，活计要大增。实话说，我要这庄子做出一份成绩来，好显显你到底是个什么样的监工。’

===Original===
“Ole Nick didn’ said nuffin but ‘Yas, suh,’ but de way he kinder grin’ ter hisse’f en show’ his big yaller teef, en snap’ de rawhide he useter kyar roun’ wid ’im, made col’ chills run up and down de backbone er dem niggers w’at heared Mars Jeems a-talkin’. En dat night dey wuz mo’nin’ en groanin’ down in de qua’ters, fer de niggers all knowed w’at wuz comin’.

“So, sho’ ’nuff, Mars Jeems went erway nex’ mawnin’, en de trouble begun. Mars Johnson sta’ted off de ve’y fus’ day fer ter see w’at he could hab ter show Mars Jeems w’en he come back. He made de tasks bigger en de rashuns littler, en w’en de niggers had wukked all day, he’d fin’ sump’n fer ’em ter do roun’ de ba’n er som’ers atter da’k, fer ter keep ’em busy a’ hour er so befo’ dey went ter sleep.

===Chinese===
“老尼克只应了声‘是，老爷’，可他那副自顾自咧嘴笑的样儿，龇出满口大黄牙，又把他惯常带在身边的那条生牛皮鞭甩得啪啪响，听得詹姆斯老爷这番话的黑奴们，个个脊梁骨上直蹿凉气。当天夜里，黑奴住区一片哀声叹气，因为黑奴们全知道要遭殃了。

“果不其然，第二天一早詹姆斯老爷走了，苦日子就开了头。头一天约翰逊老爷就开足了发条，琢磨老爷回来时能拿点什么给他看。他把定额加大，口粮缩小，黑奴们白天做满一整天，天黑之后他还能在谷仓边上什么去处寻出活来叫他们干，让他们睡前再忙活一个钟头上下。

===Original===
“About th’ee er fo’ days atter Mars Jeems went erway, young Mars Dunkin McSwayne rode up ter de big house one day wid a nigger settin’ behin’ ’im in de buggy, tied ter de seat, en ax’ ef Mars Jeems wuz home. Mars Johnson wuz at de house, and he say no.

“ ‘Well,’ sez Mars Dunkin, sezee, ‘I fotch dis nigger ober ter Mistah McLean fer ter pay a bet I made wid ’im las’ week w’en we wuz playin’ kya’ds te’gedder. I bet ’im a nigger man, en heah’s one I reckon’ll fill de bill. He wuz tuk up de yuther day fer a stray nigger, en he couldn’ gib no ’count er hisse’f, en so he wuz sol’ at oction, en I bought ’im. He’s kinder brash, but I knows yo’ powers, Mistah Johnson, en I reckon ef anybody kin make ’im toe de ma’k, you is de man.’

===Chinese===
“詹姆斯老爷走了三四天，有一天，小邓金·麦克斯韦恩老爷（Dunkin McSwayne）赶着马车来到大宅，车上还坐着个黑奴，被绑在座位上。他问詹姆斯老爷在不在家。约翰逊老爷正在宅子里，回说不在。

“‘是这样，’邓金老爷说，‘上礼拜我跟麦克莱恩先生打牌，跟他赌了一注，今天把这黑奴送来抵账。我押的是一个男黑奴，眼下这个我看正合用。前几天他当流浪黑奴叫人抓了起来，自己也说不清个来历，就给拍卖了，是我买下的。这人性子是有点冲，不过我晓得您的手段，约翰逊先生，要说谁能把他调教得服服帖帖，那就非您莫属了。’

===Original===
“Mars Johnson grin’ one er dem grins w’at show’ all his snaggle teef, en make de niggers ’low he look lack de ole debbil, en sezee ter Mars Dunkin:﻿—

“ ‘I reckon you kin trus’ me, Mistah Dunkin, fer ter tame any nigger wuz eber bawn. De nigger doan lib w’at I can’t take down in ’bout fo’ days.’

===Chinese===
“约翰逊老爷咧嘴一乐，龇出满口歪斜的牙，黑奴们都说他那样儿活像老魔王，他对邓金老爷说：

“‘这个您尽可放心，邓金先生，天底下生下来的黑奴，没有我驯不服的。但凡有个黑奴我四天里拿不下来，那他就不配活着。’

===Original===
“Well, Ole Nick had ’is han’s full long er dat noo nigger; en w’iles de res’ er de darkies wuz sorry fer de po’ man, dey ’lowed he kep’ Mars Johnson so busy dat dey got along better ’n dey’d ’a’ done ef de noo nigger had nebber come.

===Chinese===
“得，老尼克这回算是有的忙了，全亏这个新来的黑奴；其余的黑奴虽然可怜那苦命人，却也念叨说，他把约翰逊老爷缠得脱不开身，大伙的日子反倒比这个新黑奴没来时好过些。

===Original===
“De fus’ thing dat happen’, Mars Johnson sez ter dis yer noo man:﻿—

“ ‘W’at’s yo’ name, Sambo?’

“ ‘My name ain’ Sambo,’ ’spon’ de noo nigger.

===Chinese===
“头一件事，约翰逊老爷对这个新来的说：

“‘你叫什么名字，桑博（Sambo）？’

“‘我不叫桑博。’新来的黑奴应道。

===Original===
“ ‘Did I ax you w’at yo’ name wa’n’t?’ sez Mars Johnson. ‘You wants ter be pa’tic’lar how you talks ter me. Now, w’at is yo’ name, en whar did you come fum?’

“ ‘I dunno my name,’ sez de nigger, ‘en I doan ’member whar I come fum. My head is all kin’ er mix’ up.’

===Chinese===
“‘我问你你不叫什么了吗？’约翰逊老爷说，‘跟我说话，放规矩点儿。我问你，你叫什么，打哪儿来？’

“‘我不知道自己叫什么，’那黑奴说，‘也记不得打哪儿来的。我这脑子里一团乱麻。’

===Original===
“ ‘Yas,’ sez Mars Johnson, ‘I reckon I’ll ha’ ter gib you sump’n fer ter cl’ar yo’ head. At de same time, it’ll l’arn you some manners, en atter dis mebbe you’ll say “suh” w’en you speaks ter me.’

===Chinese===
“‘好啊，’约翰逊老爷说，‘看来我得给你点儿东西，把你这脑子清一清。顺便呢，也教教你规矩，往后你跟我说话，兴许就懂得叫“老爷”了。’

===Original===
“Well, Mars Johnson haul’ off wid his rawhide en hit de noo nigger once. De noo man look’ at Mars Johnson fer a minute ez ef he didn’ know w’at ter make er dis yer kin’ er l’arnin’. But w’en de oberseah raise’ his w’ip ter hit him ag’in, de noo nigger des haul’ off en made fer Mars Johnson, en ef some er de yuther niggers hadn’ stop’ ’im, it ’peared ez ef he mought ’a’ made it wa’m fer Ole Nick dere fer a w’ile. But de oberseah made de yuther niggers he’p tie de noo nigger up, en den gun ’im fo’ty, wid a dozen er so th’owed in fer good measure, fer Ole Nick wuz nebber stingy wid dem kin’ er rashuns. De nigger went on at a tarrable rate, des lack a wil’ man, but co’se he wuz bleedzd ter take his med’cine, fer he wuz tied up en couldn’ he’p hisse’f.

===Chinese===
“得，约翰逊老爷抡圆了牛皮鞭，照新来的黑奴就是一记。那新来的盯着约翰逊老爷瞧了半分钟，仿佛闹不清这一路‘教导’算个什么章程。可等监工扬鞭要抽第二下，那新来的竟抡开膀子直取约翰逊老爷，若不是旁边几个黑奴死命拦住，看那架势，老尼克一时半会儿怕要吃不了兜着走。可监工叫其余黑奴搭手，把这个新来的捆了起来，赏了他四十鞭，外饶十来鞭凑个整——老尼克发放这路口粮，向来大方得很。那黑奴破口大闹，闹得天翻地覆，活像头发了疯的野人，可他到底被捆得结结实实，动弹不得，这剂药只能乖乖受着。

===Original===
“Mars Johnson lock’ de noo nigger up in de ba’n, en didn’ gib ’im nuffin ter eat fer a day er so, ’tel he got ’im kin’er quiet’ down, en den he tu’nt ’im loose en put ’im ter wuk. De nigger ’lowed he wa’n’t useter wukkin’, en wouldn’ wuk, en Mars Johnson gun ’im anudder fo’ty fer laziness en impidence, en let ’im fas’ a day er so mo’, en den put ’im ter wuk ag’in. De nigger went ter wuk, but didn’ ’pear ter know how ter han’le a hoe. It tuk des ’bout half de oberseah’s time lookin’ atter ’im, en dat po’ nigger got mo’ lashin’s en cussin’s en cuffin’s dan any fo’ yuthers on de plantation. He didn’ mix’ wid ner talk much ter de res’ er de niggers, en couldn’ ’pear ter git it th’oo his min’ dat he wuz a slabe en had ter wuk en min’ de w’ite folks, spite er de fac’ dat Ole Nick gun ’im a lesson eve’y day. En fin’lly Mars Johnson ’lowed dat he couldn’ do nuffin wid ’im; dat ef he wuz his nigger, he’d break his sperrit er break ’is neck, one er de yuther. But co’se he wuz only sont ober on trial, en ez he didn’ gib sat’sfaction, en he hadn’ heared fum Mars Jeems ’bout w’en he wuz comin’ back; en ez he wuz feared he’d git mad some time er ’nuther en kill de nigger befo’ he knowed it, he ’lowed he’d better sen’ ’im back whar he come fum. So he tied ’im up en sont ’im back ter Mars Dunkin.

===Chinese===
“约翰逊老爷把新来的黑奴锁进谷仓，一两天不给吃食，等他多少安分了，才放出来派活。那黑奴说自己做不来活，不肯干，约翰逊老爷又赏他四十鞭，治他个又懒又犟，再饿上一天半天，然后又押去上工。黑奴是上工了，可瞧那样子竟连锄头都使得不像样。监工一半的工夫都耗在他身上，那可怜的黑奴挨的鞭子、骂声和耳刮子，比庄上随便哪四个加起来还多。他不跟旁的黑奴厮混，也不大多话，脑子里怎么也转不过这个弯——自己是个奴隶，得干活，得听白人的——任凭老尼克天天给他上一课。到末了，约翰逊老爷认了，说自己拿他没法子；说这黑奴要是归他所有，他不折了他的傲气，就折断他的脖子，二者必居其一。不过这黑奴到底是送来试工的，既然不中意，詹姆斯老爷又没捎信来说几时回来；他怕自己哪天火头上一个不留神，把黑奴当场结果了，便寻思还是把人送回原主为妙。于是他把人捆好，送还给邓金老爷了。

===Original===
“Now, Mars Dunkin McSwayne wuz one er dese yer easy-gwine gent’emen w’at didn’ lack ter hab no trouble wid niggers er nobody e’se, en he knowed ef Mars Ole Nick couldn’ git ’long wid dis nigger, nobody could. So he tuk de nigger ter town dat same day, en sol’ ’im ter a trader w’at wuz gittin’ up a gang er lackly niggers fer ter ship off on de steamboat ter go down de ribber ter Wim’l’ton en fum dere ter Noo Orleens.

===Chinese===
“这位邓金·麦克斯韦恩老爷是个好性子的绅士，不愿意跟黑奴或者任何人过不去，他也明白，要是连老尼克都处不来这个黑奴，那就没人处得来了。于是当天他就把黑奴带进城里，卖给了一个奴隶贩子。那贩子正凑一批精壮黑奴，要装上轮船顺河而下到威明顿（Wilmington），再从那儿转运新奥尔良（New Orleans）。

===Original===
“De nex’ day atter de noo man had be’n sont away, Solomon wuz wukkin’ in de cotton-fiel’, en w’en he got ter de fence nex’ ter de woods, at de een’ er de row, who sh’d he see on de yuther side but ole Aun’ Peggy. She beckon’ ter ’im﻿—de oberseah wuz down on de yuther side er de fiel’﻿—en sez she:﻿—

===Chinese===
“新来的被送走的第二天，所罗门在棉花田里做活，锄到挨着树林的篱笆、正当垄头的地方，往篱笆那头一望，谁站在那儿？不是佩姬大娘又是谁。她朝他招手——监工正在田那头——说道：

===Original===
“ ‘W’y ain’ you done come en ’po’ted ter me lack I tol’ you?’

“ ‘W’y, law! Aun’ Peggy,’ sez Solomon, ’dey ain’ nuffin ter ’po’t. Mars Jeems went away de day atter we gun ’im de goopher mixtry, en we ain’ seed hide ner hair un ’im sence, en co’se we doan know nuffin ’bout w’at ’fec’ it had on ’im.’

===Chinese===
“‘你咋不照我的话，来给我回个话儿？’

“‘哎呀，我的老天！佩姬大娘，’所罗门说，‘没啥可回的呀。我们给他下咒药的第二天，詹姆斯老爷就出了门，打那以后连他的人影儿都没瞧见，那咒在他身上到底使出个什么效力，我们自然一概不知。’

===Original===
“ ‘I doan keer nuffin ’bout yo’ Mars Jeems now; w’at I wants ter know is w’at is be’n gwine on ’mongs’ de niggers. Has you be’n gittin’ ’long any better on de plantation?’

“ ‘No, Aun’ Peggy, we be’n gittin’ ’long wusser. Mars Johnson is stric’er ’n he eber wuz befo’, en de po’ niggers doan ha’dly git time ter draw dey bref, en dey ’lows dey mought des ez well be dead ez alibe.’

===Chinese===
“‘你家詹姆斯老爷的事我这会儿不关心；我要知道的是黑奴们这头怎么样了。庄上的日子可好过些了？’

“‘没有，佩姬大娘，日子反倒更苦了。约翰逊老爷比先前管得还严，可怜的黑奴们连喘口气的工夫都捞不着，大伙都说，横竖死了活着都是一个样。’

===Original===
“ ‘Uh huh!’ sez Aun’ Peggy, sez she, ‘I tol’ you dat ’uz monst’us pow’ful goopher, en its wuk doan ’pear all at once.’

“ ‘Long ez we had dat noo nigger heah,’ Solomon went on, ‘he kep’ Mars Johnson busy pa’t er de time; but now he’s gone erway, I s’pose de res’ un us’ll ketch it wusser ’n eber.’

===Chinese===
“‘嗯哼！’佩姬大娘说，‘我不是讲了嘛，那咒厉害得很，它的功力不是一下子就显出来的。’

“‘新来的黑奴在这儿的时候，’所罗门接着说，‘还能缠住约翰逊老爷一阵子；如今他走了，只怕我们其余的人要遭更凶的罪了。’

===Original===
“ ‘W’at’s gone wid de noo nigger?’ sez Aun’ Peggy, rale quick, battin’ her eyes en straight’nin’ up.

“ ‘Ole Nick done sont ’im back ter Mars Dunkin, who had fotch ’im heah fer ter pay a gamblin’ debt ter Mars Jeems,’ sez Solomon, ‘en I heahs Mars Dunkin has sol’ ’im ter a nigger-trader up in Patesville, w’at’s gwine ter ship ’im off wid a gang ter-morrer.’

===Chinese===
“‘新来的黑奴怎么着了？’佩姬大娘忙问，眼睛一眨一眨，直起了身子。

“‘老尼克把他送回邓金老爷那儿去了，他本来就是邓金老爷带来抵赌债的，’所罗门说，‘我听说邓金老爷已经把他卖给了帕茨维尔城里的一个奴隶贩子，明天就要连人带一批黑奴装船运走了。’

===Original===
“Ole Aun’ Peggy ’peared ter git rale stirred up w’en Solomon tol’ ’er dat, en sez she, shakin’ her stick at ’im:﻿—

===Chinese===
“所罗门这话一出，佩姬大娘像是当真动了气，她拿手里的棍子指着他，说：

===Original===
“ ‘W’y didn’ you come en tell me ’bout dis noo nigger bein’ sol’ erway? Didn’ you promus me, ef I’d gib you dat goopher, you’d come en ’po’t ter me ’bout all w’at wuz gwine on on dis plantation? Co’se I could ’a’ foun’ out fer myse’f, but I ’pended on yo’ tellin’ me, en now by not doin’ it I’s feared you gwine spile my cunj’in’. You come down ter my house ter-night en do w’at I tells you, er I’ll put a spell on you dat’ll make yo’ ha’r fall out so you’ll be bal’, en yo’ eyes drap out so you can’t see, en yo teef fall out so you can’t eat, en yo’ years grow up so you can’t heah. W’en you is foolin’ wid a cunjuh ’oman lack me, you got ter min’ yo’ P’s en Q’s er dey’ll be trouble sho’ ’nuff.’

===Chinese===
“‘新来的黑奴给卖掉了，这么大的事，你咋不来告诉我？你不是应承过我，只要我把咒给了你，庄上有什么动静你都来回报？我自然有法子自家打听，可我一心指望你说，如今你误了事，我怕是要坏了我的法术。今儿夜里你到我家来，我叫你做什么就做什么，不然我就给你念一道咒，叫你头发掉个精光，秃成个光瓢；眼珠子掉出来，叫你瞧不见路；牙齿掉个干净，叫你吃不成饭；耳朵眼儿封起来，叫你听不见声儿。跟我这号巫女打交道，你可得处处小心着点儿，不然准有你的好果子吃。’

===Original===
“So co’se Solomon went down ter Aun’ Peggy’s dat night, en she gun ’im a roasted sweet’n’ ’tater.

===Chinese===
“所罗门自然当夜就去了佩姬大娘家，大娘给了他一只烤红薯。

===Original===
“ ‘You take dis yer sweet’n’ ’tater,’ sez she﻿—‘I done goophered it ’speshly fer dat noo nigger, so you better not eat it yo’se’f er you’ll wush you hadn’﻿—en slip off ter town, en fin’ dat strange man, en gib ’im dis yer sweet’n’ ’tater. He mus’ eat it befo’ mawnin’, sho’, ef he doan wanter be sol’ erway ter Noo Orleens.’

“ ‘But s’posen de patteroles ketch me, Aun’ Peggy, w’at I gwine ter do?’ sez Solomon.

===Chinese===
“‘你带上这只红薯，’她说——‘我是特意给那个新来的黑奴下的咒，你自个儿可千万别嘴馋偷吃，不然有你后悔的——悄悄溜进城去，找到那个外乡人，把红薯给他。天亮之前他务必吃下去，错不了，不然他就要给卖到新奥尔良去了。’

“‘可万一巡逻队（patteroles）把我逮住，佩姬大娘，我咋办？’所罗门说。

===Original===
“ ‘De patteroles ain’ gwine tech you, but ef you doan fin’ dat nigger, *I’m* gwine git you, en you’ll fin’ me wusser ’n de patteroles. Des hol’ on a minute, en I’ll sprinkle you wid some er dis mixtry out’n dis yer bottle, so de patteroles can’t see you, en you kin rub yo’ feet wid some er dis yer grease out’n dis go’d, so you kin run fas’, en rub some un it on yo’ eyes so you kin see in de da’k; en den you mus’ fin’ dat noo nigger en gib ’im dis yer ’tater, er you gwine ter hab mo’ trouble on yo’ han’s ’n you eber had befo’ in yo’ life er eber will hab sence.’

===Chinese===
“‘巡逻队沾不着你的边儿；可你要是找不着那黑奴，*我*可要来找你，到时候你就知道，我比巡逻队还难对付。且站住一会儿，我用这瓶子里的药水给你洒一洒，巡逻队就瞧不见你；你再用这葫芦里的油膏抹抹脚板，好叫你跑得飞快；再抹一星到眼皮上，好叫你黑地里也瞧得见；然后你就去寻那个新来的黑奴，把红薯给他吃下去。不然哪，你这辈子从前受过的罪、往后要受的罪，全加在一块儿，也没这一回的大。’

===Original===
“So Solomon tuk de sweet’n’ ’tater en sta’ted up de road fas’ ez he could go, en befo’ long he retch’ town. He went right ’long by de patteroles, en dey didn’ ’pear ter notice ’im, en bimeby he foun’ whar de strange nigger was kep’, en he walked right pas’ de gyard at de do’ en foun’ ’im. De nigger couldn’ see ’im, ob co’se, en he couldn’ ’a’ seed de nigger in de da’k, ef it hadn’ be’n fer de stuff Aun’ Peggy gun ’im ter rub on ’is eyes. De nigger wuz layin’ in a co’nder, ’sleep, en Solomon des slip’ up ter ’im, en hilt dat sweet’n’ ’tater ’fo’ de nigger’s nose, en he des nach’ly retch’ up wid his han’, en tuk de ’tater en eat it in his sleep, widout knowin’ it. Wen’ Solomon seed he’d done eat de ’tater, he went back en tol’ Aun’ Peggy, en den went home ter his cabin ter sleep, ’way ’long ’bout two o’clock in de mawnin’.

===Chinese===
“于是所罗门揣上红薯，顺着大道撒开腿能跑多快跑多快，没多久就进了城。他从巡逻队跟前大摇大摆走过，他们竟像没瞧见他似的；不多时他寻着了那个外乡黑奴关押的地方，径直打门口的看守面前走过去，找到了他。那黑奴自然瞧不见他；若不是有佩姬大娘给他抹眼睛的药，黑灯瞎火里他也瞧不见那黑奴。黑奴正蜷在角落里睡觉，所罗门悄悄凑上去，把那只红薯凑到他鼻子底下，那黑奴迷迷糊糊伸手一抓，接过去竟在睡梦里吃掉了，自家浑然不知。所罗门见红薯下了肚，便回去报与佩姬大娘，然后回自家小屋睡觉去了，那都后半夜两点来钟了。

===Original===
“De nex’ day wuz Sunday, en so de niggers had a little time ter deyse’ves. Solomon wuz kinder ’sturb’ in his min’ thinkin’ ’bout his junesey w’at ’uz gone away, en wond’rin’ w’at Aun’ Peggy had ter do wid dat noo nigger; en he had sa’ntered up in de woods so ’s ter be by hisse’f a little, en at de same time ter look atter a rabbit-trap he’d sot down in de aidge er de swamp, w’en who sh’d he see stan’in’ unner a tree but a w’ite man.

===Chinese===
“第二天是礼拜天，黑奴们难得有一点自家的工夫。所罗门心里烦乱，一头惦着被送走的心上人，一头纳闷佩姬大娘跟那新来的黑奴能有什么牵扯；他信步踱进树林，想独自清静一会儿，顺便照看一眼下在沼泽边上的兔子夹子，一抬眼，树底下站着的，竟是个白人。

===Original===
“Solomon didn’ knowed de w’ite man at fus’, ’tel de w’ite man spoke up ter ’im.

“ ‘Is dat you, Solomon?’ sezee.

===Chinese===
“起初所罗门没认出那白人，直到白人先开了口。

“‘是你吗，所罗门？’他说。

===Original===
“Den Solomon reco’nized de voice.

“ ‘Fer de Lawd’s sake, Mars Jeems! is dat you?’

===Chinese===
“所罗门这才听出了那声音。

“‘老天爷在上，詹姆斯老爷！真是您吗？’

===Original===
“ ‘Yas, Solomon,’ sez his marster, ‘dis is me, er w’at’s lef’ er me.’

===Chinese===
“‘是我，所罗门，’他的主人说，‘是我——或者说，是我剩下的这一点儿。’

===Original===
“It wa’n’t no wonder Solomon hadn’ knowed Mars Jeems at fus’, fer he wuz dress’ lack a po’ w’ite man, en wuz barefooted, en look’ monst’us pale en peaked, ez ef he’d des come th’oo a ha’d spell er sickness.

===Chinese===
“也难怪所罗门起初没认出詹姆斯老爷，他穿得像个穷白人，赤着双脚，脸色惨淡枯槁，像是一场大病才起的人。

===Original===
“ ‘You er lookin’ kinder po’ly, Mars Jeems,’ sez Solomon. ‘Is you be’n sick, suh?’

===Chinese===
“‘您气色不大好，詹姆斯老爷，’所罗门说，‘您是病了吗，老爷？’

===Original===
“ ‘No, Solomon,’ sez Mars Jeems, shakin’ his head, en speakin’ sorter slow en sad, ‘I ain’ be’n sick, but I’s had a monst’us bad dream﻿—fac’, a reg’lar, nach’ul nightmare. But tell me how things has be’n gwine on up ter de plantation sence I be’n gone, Solomon.’

===Chinese===
“‘没有，所罗门，’詹姆斯老爷摇着头，话说得又慢又凄然，‘我不曾生病，只是做了个顶顶凶的梦——实实在在，是一场不折不扣、如假包换的噩梦。不过先跟我说说，我不在的这些日子，庄上情形怎么样，所罗门。’

===Original===
“So Solomon up en tol’ ’im ’bout de craps, en ’bout de hosses en de mules, en ’bout de cows en de hawgs. En w’en he ’mence’ ter tell ’bout de noo nigger, Mars Jeems prick’ up ’is yeahs en listen’, en eve’y now en den he’d say, ‘Uh huh! uh huh!’ en nod ’is head. En bimeby, w’en he’d ax’ Solomon some mo’ queshtuns, he sez, sezee:﻿—

===Chinese===
“所罗门便一五一十跟他讲起庄稼，讲马啊骡子啊，讲牛啊猪啊。等讲到那个新来的黑奴，詹姆斯老爷竖起耳朵听着，时不时应一声‘嗯哼！嗯哼！’，点一点头。过了半晌，他又盘问了所罗门几句，然后说道：

===Original===
“ ‘Now, Solomon, I doan want you ter say a wo’d ter nobody ’bout meetin’ me heah, but I wants you ter slip up ter de house, en fetch me some clo’s en some shoes﻿—I fergot ter tell you dat a man rob’ me back yander on de road en swap’ clo’s wid me widout axin’ me whuther er no﻿—but you neenter say nuffin ’bout dat, nuther. You go en fetch me some clo’s heah, so nobody won’t see you, en keep yo’ mouf shet, en I’ll gib you a dollah.’

===Chinese===
“‘听着，所罗门，在这儿碰见我的事，一个字也不许对外人讲；可我要你悄悄溜回宅子，给我取几件衣裳、一双鞋来——忘了告诉你，方才在路上有人劫了我，没问我愿不愿意，就跟我把衣裳换了——不过这件事你也一字别提。你去把衣裳给我取到这儿来，别叫人撞见，嘴闭严实，我给你一块钱。’

===Original===
“Solomon wuz so ’stonish’ he lack ter fell ober in his tracks, w’en Mars Jeems promus’ ter gib ’im a dollah. Dey su’t’nly wuz a change come ober Mars Jeems, w’en he offer’ one er his niggers dat much money. Solomon ’mence’ ter ’spec’ dat Aun’ Peggy’s cunj’ation had be’n wukkin’ monst’us strong.

===Chinese===
“詹姆斯老爷许他一块钱，所罗门惊得差点当场栽个跟头。肯赏自家黑奴这么一大笔钱，詹姆斯老爷身上准是起了什么大变化。所罗门开始疑心，佩姬大娘那场巫术，只怕灵验得出奇。

===Original===
“Solomon fotch Mars Jeems some clo’s en shoes, en dat same eb’nin’ Mars Jeems ’peared at de house, en let on lack he des dat minute got home fum Robeson County. Mars Johnson was all ready ter talk ter ’im, but Mars Jeems sont ’im wo’d he wa’n’t feelin’ ve’y well dat night, en he’d see ’im ter-morrer.

===Chinese===
“所罗门给詹姆斯老爷取来了衣裳鞋袜。当天傍晚，詹姆斯老爷现身大宅，装出刚从罗伯逊县到家的那副样子。约翰逊老爷早候着要向他禀报，詹姆斯老爷却捎话说，今儿晚上身上不爽快，明儿再见。

===Original===
“So nex’ mawnin’ atter breakfus’ Mars Jeems sont fer de oberseah, en ax’ ’im fer ter gib ’count er his styoa’dship. Ole Nick tol’ Mars Jeems how much wuk be’n done, en got de books en showed ’im how much money be’n save’. Den Mars Jeems ax’ ’im how de darkies be’n behabin’, en Mars Johnson say dey be’n behabin’ good, most un ’em, en dem w’at didn’ behabe good at fus’ change dey conduc’ atter he got holt un ’em a time er two.

===Chinese===
“第二天一早，用过早饭，詹姆斯老爷把监工叫来，要他把这一个月的经管交代清楚。老尼克报给詹姆斯老爷做了多少活计，又取来账本，给他瞧省下了多少钱。詹姆斯老爷再问黑奴们表现怎么样，约翰逊老爷说大都还好，头茬儿不听话的，经他收拾过一两回，也都改了行止。

===Original===
“ ‘All,’ sezee, ‘ ’cep’n’ de noo nigger Mistah Dunkin fotch ober heah en lef’ on trial, w’iles you wuz gone.’

“ ‘Oh, yas,’ ’lows Mars Jeems, ‘tell me all ’bout dat noo nigger. I heared a little ’bout dat quare noo nigger las’ night, en it wuz des too redik’lus. Tell me all ’bout dat noo nigger.’

===Chinese===
“‘全都还乖，’他说，‘只有邓金先生趁您不在家送来试工的那个新黑奴除外。’

“‘哦，是吗，’詹姆斯老爷慢悠悠地说，‘那就把那个新黑奴的事，一五一十都讲给我听。昨儿晚上我听了点儿那古怪新黑奴的传闻，简直太可乐了。把那个新黑奴的事都讲给我听。’

===Original===
“So seein’ Mars Jeems so good-na-chu’d ’bout it, Mars Johnson up en tol’ ’im how he tied up de noo han’ de fus’ day en gun ’im fo’ty ’ca’se he wouldn’ tell ’im ’is name.

“ ‘Ha, ha, ha!’ sez Mars Jeems, laffin’ fit ter kill, ‘but dat is too funny fer any use. Tell me some mo’ ’bout dat noo nigger.’

===Chinese===
“见詹姆斯老爷兴致这么好，约翰逊老爷便讲起来，说头一天怎么把那新来的捆起来，赏了四十鞭，治他不肯报名字的罪。

“‘哈，哈，哈！’詹姆斯老爷笑得直不起腰，‘这可太好笑了，好笑得没治了。再讲讲那个新黑奴的事。’

===Original===
“So Mars Johnson went on en tol’ ’im how he had ter starbe de noo nigger ’fo’ he could make ’im take holt er a hoe.

“ ‘Dat wuz de beatinis’ notion fer a nigger,’ sez Mars Jeems, ‘puttin’ on airs, des lack he wuz a w’ite man! En I reckon you didn’ do nuffin ter ’im?’

===Chinese===
“约翰逊老爷接着讲，说他怎么非得把那新黑奴饿上几顿，才叫他肯拿锄头。

“‘一个黑奴，居然摆起这么大的架子，’詹姆斯老爷说，‘还真把自家当白人了！我谅你也没轻饶他吧？’

===Original===
“ ‘Oh, no, suh,’ sez de oberseah, grinnin’ lack a chessy-cat, ‘I didn’ do nuffin but take de hide off’n ’im.’

“Mars Jeems lafft en lafft, ’tel it ’peared lack he wuz des gwine ter bu’st. ‘*Tell* me some mo’ ’bout dat noo nigger, oh, *tell* me some mo’. Dat noo nigger int’rusts me, he do, en dat is a fac’.’

===Chinese===
“‘嗐，没轻饶，老爷，’监工咧着嘴笑，活像只柴郡猫，‘我不过揭了他一层皮。’

“詹姆斯老爷笑了又笑，直笑得像要背过气去。‘*讲讲*那个新黑奴还有什么事，哦，再*讲讲*。这新黑奴真对我的心思，真的，不骗你。’

===Original===
“Mars Johnson didn’ quite un’erstan’ w’y Mars Jeems sh’d make sich a great ’miration ’bout de noo nigger, but co’se he want’ ter please de gent’eman w’at hi’ed ’im, en so he ’splain’ all ’bout how many times he had ter cowhide de noo nigger, en how he made ’im do tasks twicet ez big ez some er de yuther han’s, en how he’d chain ’im up in de ba’n at night en feed ’im on co’n-bread en water.

===Chinese===
“约翰逊老爷闹不明白，詹姆斯老爷干嘛对这个新黑奴这么大惊小怪，可他自然想讨雇主的欢心，便细细交代起来：他不得不拿牛皮鞭抽了那黑奴多少回，怎么派他干双倍于旁人的定额，夜里怎么把他拴锁在谷仓里，只给玉米面包和白水吃。

===Original===
“ ‘Oh! but you is a monst’us good oberseah; you is de bes’ oberseah in dis county, Mistah Johnson,’ sez Mars Jeems, w’en de oberseah got th’oo wid his tale; ‘en dey ain’ nebber be’n no nigger-breaker lack you roun’ heah befo’. En you desarbes great credit fer sendin’ dat nigger ’way befo’ you sp’ilt ’im fer de market. Fac’, you is sech a monst’us good oberseah, en you is got dis yer plantation in sech fine shape, dat I reckon I doan need you no mo’. You is got dese yer darkies so well train’ dat I ’spec’ I kin run ’em myse’f fum dis time on. But I does wush you had ’a’ hilt on ter dat noo nigger ’tel I got home, fer I’d ’a’ lack ter ’a’ seed ’im, I su’t’nly should.’

===Chinese===
“‘哎呀！你可真是个了不得的好监工，是全县头一号的好监工，约翰逊先生，’等监工讲完，詹姆斯老爷说，‘这一带从来没出过像你这样的驯奴行家。你没等把那黑奴糟蹋到卖不出价，就把他先送走了，这份功劳实在不小。实话说，你这么个了不得的好监工，又把这庄子整治得这么兴旺，我看我往后用不着你了。你把这些黑奴调教得这么服帖，往后我自己就支使得动了。只可惜你没把那新黑奴留到我回家，我还真想会一会他呢，千真万确想见。’

===Original===
“De oberseah wuz so ’stonish’ he didn’ ha’dly know w’at ter say, but fin’lly he ax’ Mars Jeems ef he wouldn’ gib’im a riccommen’ fer ter git ernudder place.

“ ‘No, suh,’ sez Mars Jeems, ‘somehow er ’nuther I doan lack yo’ looks sence I come back dis time, en I’d much ruther you wouldn’ stay roun’ heah. Fac’, I’s feared ef I’d meet you alone in de woods some time, I mought wanter ha’m you. But layin’ dat aside, I be’n lookin’ ober dese yer books er yo’n w’at you kep’ w’iles I wuz ’way, en fer a yeah er so back, en dere’s some figgers w’at ain’ des cl’ar ter me. I ain’ got no time fer ter talk ’bout ’em now, but I ’spec’ befo’ I settles wid you fer dis las’ mont’, you better come up heah ter-morrer, atter I’s look’ de books en ’counts ober some mo’, en den we’ll straighten ou’ business all up.’

===Chinese===
“监工惊得半晌说不出话，末了才问詹姆斯老爷，能不能给他写封荐书，好另谋一处差事。

“‘不行，’詹姆斯老爷说，‘也不知怎么，这回回来，我怎么看你怎么不顺眼，你顶好别在这儿一带待着。不瞒你说，我怕哪天在林子里跟你单撞上，一时忍不住要伤了你。这话搁下不提——我不在家的日子连同往前一年上下，你记的这些账我翻了一翻，里头有几个数目我瞧着不大清爽。这会儿没工夫跟你掰扯，不过依我看，等这最后一个月跟你结账之前，你顶好明儿再过来一趟，容我把这些账本账目再细细核过，咱们把账务从头理个干干净净。’

===Original===
“Mars Jeems ’lowed atterwa’ds dat he wuz des shootin’ in de da’k w’en he said dat ’bout de books, but howsomeber, Mars Nick Johnson lef’ dat naberhood ’twix’ de nex’ two suns, en nobody roun’ dere nebber seed hide ner hair un ’im sence. En all de darkies t’ank de Lawd, en ’lowed it wuz a good riddance er bad rubbage.

===Chinese===
“詹姆斯老爷后来说，他讲那番账目的话，原是黑地里放枪——瞎诈唬罢了。可不管怎样，尼克·约翰逊老爷没等再出两个日头，就离开了那片地界，那一带从此再没人见过他的影儿。黑奴们个个感谢上帝，都说这叫祸害清了场，甩得好。

===Original===
“But all dem things I done tol’ you ain’ nuffin ’side’n de change w’at come ober Mars Jeems fum dat time on. Aun’ Peggy’s goopher had made a noo man un ’im enti’ely. De nex’ day atter he come back, he tol’ de han’s dey neenter wuk on’y fum sun ter sun, en he cut dey tasks down so dey didn’ nobody hab ter stan’ ober ’em wid a rawhide er a hick’ry. En he ’lowed ef de niggers want ter hab a dance in de big ba’n any Sad’day night, dey mought hab it. En bimeby, w’en Solomon seed how good Mars Jeems wuz, he ax’ ’im ef he wouldn’ please sen’ down ter de yuther plantation fer his junesey. Mars Jeems say su’t’nly, en gun Solomon a pass en a note ter de oberseah on de yuther plantation, en sont Solomon down ter Robeson County wid a hoss en buggy fer ter fetch his junesey back. Wen’ de niggers see how fine Mars Jeems gwine treat ’em, dey all tuk ter sweethea’tin’ en juneseyin’ en singin’ en dancin’, en eight er ten couples got married, en bimeby eve’ybody ’mence’ ter say Mars Jeems McLean got a finer plantation, en slicker-lookin’ niggers, en dat he ’uz makin’ mo’ cotton en co’n, dan any yuther gent’eman in de county. En Mars Jeems’s own junesey, Miss Libbie, heared ’bout de noo gwines-on on Mars Jeems’s plantation, en she change’ her min’ ’bout Mars Jeems en tuk ’im back ag’in, en ’fo’ long dey had a fine weddin’, en all de darkies had a big feas’, en dey wuz fiddlin’ en dancin’ en funnin’ en frolic’in’ fum sundown ’tel mawnin’.”

===Chinese===
“可我跟你讲的这些，比起打那以后詹姆斯老爷身上起的变化，全不算什么了。佩姬大娘那道咒，把他彻头彻尾换成了一个新人。回来后的第二天，他就传话给众工人，往后只消日出而作、日落而息，又把定额减下来，再不用谁攥着生牛皮鞭或者山核桃棍在旁边督着。他还说，黑奴们哪个礼拜六晚上想在大谷仓里开个舞会，只管去开。后来所罗门见詹姆斯老爷这么和善，便求他行个好，把他的心上人从那处种植园接回来。詹姆斯老爷一口应承，给所罗门开了通行证，又给那边的监工捎了便条，打发所罗门赶着马车主下罗伯逊县，把他的心上人接了回来。黑奴们见詹姆斯老爷这么厚待他们，便都谈起情、说起爱、唱起歌、跳起舞来，成了亲的有八九对；不多时，人人都说詹姆斯·麦克莱恩老爷的庄子是全县最兴旺的，黑奴个个光鲜体面，棉花玉米也出得比哪位绅士家都多。连詹姆斯老爷自个儿的心上人利比小姐，也听说了他庄子上的新气象，回心转意，重新接纳了他；没过多久，两人办了场体面的婚礼，黑奴们大摆宴席，拉琴的拉琴，跳舞的跳舞，打趣的打趣，热闹个不停，从日头落山一直闹到大天亮。”

===Original===
“And they all lived happy ever after,” I said, as the old man reached a full stop.

===Chinese===
“往后他们就永远幸福地生活在一起了。”老人把故事讲到了头，我说道。

===Original===
“Yas, suh,” he said, interpreting my remarks as a question, “dey did. Solomon useter say,” he added, “dat Aun’ Peggy’s goopher had turnt Mars Jeems ter a nigger, en dat dat noo han’ wuz Mars Jeems hisse’f. But co’se Solomon didn’ das’ ter let on ’bout w’at he ’spicioned, en ole Aun’ Peggy would ’a’ ’nied it ef she had be’n ax’, fer she’d ’a’ got in trouble sho’, ef it ’uz knowed she’d be’n cunj’in’ de w’ite folks.

===Chinese===
“是，先生，”他说，把我的话当成了发问，“他们后来是那样。所罗门从前常念叨，”他补了一句，“说佩姬大娘那道咒把詹姆斯老爷变成了个黑奴，那个新来的工人就是詹姆斯老爷本人。不过所罗门自然不敢说破他心里疑心的那些话，老佩姬大娘呢，就是有人问起，也准会一口否认，因为要是叫人知道她给白人施过咒，那她准没好果子吃。

===Original===
“Dis yer tale goes ter show,” concluded Julius sententiously, as the man came up and announced that the spring was ready for us to get water, “dat w’ite folks w’at is so ha’d en stric’, en doan make no ’lowance fer po’ ign’ant niggers w’at ain’ had no chanst ter l’arn, is li’ble ter hab bad dreams, ter say de leas’, en dat dem w’at is kin’ en good ter po’ people is sho’ ter prosper en git ’long in de worl’.”

===Chinese===
“这个故事说明，”正巧那人走上前来，说泉眼收拾好了，可以灌水了，朱利叶斯一本正经地总结道，“白人要是那么狠、那么苛，全不体恤那些没机会受教化的可怜无知黑人，轻则也要摊上噩梦；而那些心慈面软、善待穷苦人的，就准定兴旺发达，在这世上顺顺当当。”

===Original===
“That is a very strange story, Uncle Julius,” observed my wife, smiling, “and Solomon’s explanation is quite improbable.”

“Yes, Julius,” said I, “that was powerful goopher. I am glad, too, that you told us the moral of the story; it might have escaped us otherwise. By the way, did you make that up all by yourself?”

===Chinese===
“这故事可真够离奇的，朱利叶斯大叔，”我妻子含着笑说，“所罗门那套解释也实在难以叫人相信。”

“是啊，朱利叶斯，”我说，“那可真是厉害的咒。还得谢谢你把故事的道理点给我们听，不然我们兴许就错过了。对了，这故事是你自个儿现编的吗？”

===Original===
The old man’s face assumed an injured look, expressive more of sorrow than of anger, and shaking his head he replied:﻿—

===Chinese===
老人脸上摆出一副受了委屈的神情，与其说是恼怒，不如说是伤心，他摇着头答道：

===Original===
“No, suh, I heared dat tale befo’ you er Mis’ Annie dere wuz bawn, suh. My mammy tol’ me dat tale w’en I wa’n’t mo’ d’n knee-high ter a hopper-grass.”

===Chinese===
“不是，先生，这故事打您跟那边的安妮太太还没出娘胎俺就听过了，先生。是俺妈妈讲给俺听的，那会儿俺才到蚂蚱膝盖那么高哩。”

===Original===
I drove to town next morning, on some business, and did not return until noon; and after dinner I had to visit a neighbor, and did not get back until suppertime. I was smoking a cigar on the back piazza in the early evening, when I saw a familiar figure carrying a bucket of water to the barn. I called my wife.

“My dear,” I said severely, “what is that rascal doing here? I thought I discharged him yesterday for good and all.”

===Chinese===
第二天上午我进城办事，到晌午才回来；午饭后又不得不去拜访一位邻居，直到晚饭时分才到家。天刚擦黑那阵儿，我正在屋后的游廊上抽雪茄，忽然瞧见一个熟悉的身影提着一桶水往谷仓去。我叫来了妻子。

“亲爱的，”我板起脸说，“那个捣蛋鬼在这儿干什么？我记得昨天已经把他一劳永逸地辞退了。”

===Original===
“Oh, yes,” she answered, “I forgot to tell you. He was hanging round the place all the morning, and looking so down in the mouth, that I told him that if he would try to do better, we would give him one more chance. He seems so grateful, and so really in earnest in his promises of amendment, that I’m sure you’ll not regret taking him back.”

I was seriously enough annoyed to let my cigar go out. I did not share my wife’s rose-colored hopes in regard to Tom; but as I did not wish the servants to think there was any conflict of authority in the household, I let the boy stay.

===Chinese===
“哦，对了，”她答道，“我忘了告诉你。他一上午都在这宅子附近转悠，一副垂头丧气的可怜相，我就跟他说，要是他肯努力改好，我们就再给他一次机会。他看上去那么知恩，改过的保证又那么实心实意，我相信你把他留下来，一定不会后悔的。”

我心里烦得够呛，竟由着雪茄自个儿熄了。对汤姆，我可不像妻子那样抱着玫瑰色的指望；但我不愿让仆人们觉得这个家里号令不一，便让那孩子留了下来。

## The Conjurer’s Revenge / 巫师的复仇

===Original===
Sunday was sometimes a rather dull day at our place. In the morning, when the weather was pleasant, my wife and I would drive to town, a distance of about five miles, to attend the church of our choice. The afternoons we spent at home, for the most part, occupying ourselves with the newspapers and magazines, and the contents of a fairly good library. We had a piano in the house, on which my wife played with skill and feeling. I possessed a passable baritone voice, and could accompany myself indifferently well when my wife was not by to assist me. When these resources failed us, we were apt to find it a little dull.

One Sunday afternoon in early spring﻿—the balmy spring of North Carolina, when the air is in that ideal balance between heat and cold where one wishes it could always remain﻿—my wife and I were seated on the front piazza, she wearily but conscientiously ploughing through a missionary report, while I followed the impossible career of the blonde heroine of a rudimentary novel. I had thrown the book aside in disgust, when I saw Julius coming through the yard, under the spreading elms, which were already in full leaf. He wore his Sunday clothes, and advanced with a dignity of movement quite different from his weekday slouch.

===Chinese===
我们家的礼拜天，有时是相当沉闷的一天。上午天气好的时候，我和妻子会赶车进城——大约五英里的路程——去我们选定的那家教堂做礼拜。下午我们多半待在家里，靠报纸杂志和一座还算像样的藏书打发时光。屋里有一架钢琴，妻子弹得娴熟而有情致。我呢，有一副还过得去的男中音嗓子，妻子不在旁边替我伴奏的时候，也能自己给自己弹得马马虎虎。这些消遣一旦用尽，我们便往往觉得日子有点百无聊赖。

早春时节的一个礼拜天下午——北卡罗来纳和煦的春天，空气正处于不冷不热、恰到好处的理想平衡里，叫人巴不得它一年到头都这样——我和妻子坐在屋前的游廊上：她一脸倦意，却仍尽责地啃着一份传教报告，我在追一部入门小说里那位金发女主角那叫人没法信的际遇。我刚把那本书厌恶地撇在一边，就望见朱利叶斯穿过院子走过来，走在枝叶已然亭亭如盖的榆树底下。他穿着过礼拜天的体面衣裳，步态从容庄重，和平日干活时那副懒散模样判若两人。

===Original===
“Have a seat, Julius,” I said, pointing to an empty rocking-chair.

“No, thanky, boss, I’ll des set here on de top step.”

“Oh, no, Uncle Julius,” exclaimed Annie, “take this chair. You will find it much more comfortable.”

The old man grinned in appreciation of her solicitude, and seated himself somewhat awkwardly.

“Julius,” I remarked, “I am thinking of setting out scuppernong vines on that sand-hill where the three persimmon-trees are; and while I’m working there, I think I’ll plant watermelons between the vines, and get a little something to pay for my first year’s work. The new railroad will be finished by the middle of summer, and I can ship the melons North, and get a good price for them.”

===Chinese===
「坐吧，朱利叶斯。」我指着一旁的空摇椅说。

「不啦，多谢您呐，老板，俺就坐这最上头一级台阶得啦。」

「哎，那怎么行，朱利叶斯大叔，」安妮忙说，「坐这把椅子吧，这把舒服得多。」

老人咧嘴一笑，领受了她这番好意，随后略带拘谨地坐了下来。

「朱利叶斯，」我说，「我打算在那片长着三棵柿子树的沙冈上栽种斯卡珀农葡萄藤；趁在那边耕作的当儿，我想在葡萄藤之间再种上西瓜，头一年的辛苦也好换回一点进项。新铁路到仲夏就能通车，我可以把西瓜运到北方去卖，卖个好价钱。」

===Original===
“Ef you er gwine ter hab any mo’ ploughin’ ter do,” replied Julius, “I ’spec’ you’ll ha’ ter buy ernudder creetur, ’ca’se hit’s much ez dem hosses kin do ter ’ten’ ter de wuk dey got now.”

“Yes, I had thought of that. I think I’ll get a mule; a mule can do more work, and doesn’t require as much attention as a horse.”

“I wouldn’ ’vise you ter buy no mule,” remarked Julius, with a shake of his head.

“Why not?”

===Chinese===
「您要是往后还有地要犁，」朱利叶斯答道，「俺寻思您还得再添一头牲口，因为眼下那几匹马应付手里的活计，已经是勉力支撑了。」

「是啊，这个我也想到了。我看我就买头骡子吧；骡子干的活多，又不像马那样费人照料。」

「俺可不敢劝您买骡子。」朱利叶斯说着摇了摇头。

「为什么？」

===Original===
“Well, you may ’low hit’s all foolis’ness, but ef I wuz in yo’ place, I wouldn’ buy no mule.”

“But that isn’t a reason; what objection have you to a mule?”

“Fac’ is,” continued the old man, in a serious tone, “I doan lack ter dribe a mule. I’s alluz afeared I mought be imposin’ on some human creetur; eve’y time I cuts a mule wid a hick’ry, ’pears ter me mos’ lackly I’s cuttin’ some er my own relations, er somebody e’se w’at can’t he’p deyse’ves.”

===Chinese===
「唉，您也许要当这是糊涂话，可俺要是换在您的位子上，俺决不买骡子。」

「可这不算个理由；你到底为什么反对骡子？」

「实情是，」老人换上一本正经的口气接着说，「俺不愿赶骡子。俺老是提心吊胆，怕自己是在欺负哪个活人；俺每回举起山核桃木棍抽骡子，总觉得十有八九，是抽在了自家哪个亲眷身上，要不就是哪个没法替自己做主的旁人身上。」

===Original===
“What put such an absurd idea into your head?” I asked.

My question was followed by a short silence, during which Julius seemed engaged in a mental struggle.

“I dunno ez hit’s wuf w’ile ter tell you dis,” he said, at length. “I doan ha’dly ’spec’ fer you ter b’lieve it. Does you ’member dat club-footed man w’at hilt de hoss fer you de yuther day w’en you was gittin’ out’n de rockaway down ter Mars Archie McMillan’s sto’?”

===Chinese===
「这么个荒唐念头，是怎么钻进你脑袋里去的？」我问。

我这一问之后，是短短一阵沉默，这工夫朱利叶斯仿佛在进行一场内心的挣扎。

「这事值不值得说给您听，俺拿不准，」他终于开了口。「俺不大指望您肯信。您还记得前几天在阿奇·麦克米伦（Archie McMillan）老爷的铺子门前，您从四轮敞篷马车上下来那会儿，替您牵住马的那个拐脚汉子吗？」

===Original===
“Yes, I believe I do remember seeing a club-footed man there.”

“Did you eber see a club-footed nigger befo’ er sence?”

“No, I can’t remember that I ever saw a club-footed colored man,” I replied, after a moment’s reflection.

“You en Mis’ Annie wouldn’ wanter b’lieve me, ef I wuz ter ’low dat dat man was oncet a mule?”

===Chinese===
「记得，我想我是记得在那儿见过一个拐脚的人。」

「您从前还是打那以后，可曾见过第二个拐脚的黑人？」

「没有，我想不起来见过哪个拐脚的有色人种。」我想了片刻，答道。

「俺要是说，那个人从前有一阵子是头骡子，您和安妮太太还肯信俺么？」

===Original===
“No,” I replied, “I don’t think it very likely that you could make us believe it.”

“Why, Uncle Julius!” said Annie severely, “what ridiculous nonsense!”

This reception of the old man’s statement reduced him to silence, and it required some diplomacy on my part to induce him to vouchsafe an explanation. The prospect of a long, dull afternoon was not alluring, and I was glad to have the monotony of Sabbath quiet relieved by a plantation legend.

“W’en I wuz a young man,” began Julius, when I had finally prevailed upon him to tell us the story, “dat club-footed nigger﻿—his name is Primus﻿—use’ ter b’long ter ole Mars Jim McGee ober on de Lumbe’ton plank-road. I use’ ter go ober dere ter see a ’oman w’at libbed on de plantation; dat’s how I come ter know all erbout it. Dis yer Primus wuz de livelies’ han’ on de place, alluz a-dancin’, en drinkin’, en runnin’ roun’, en singin’, en pickin’ de banjo; ’cep’n’ once in a w’ile, w’en he’d ’low he wa’n’t treated right ’bout sump’n ernudder, he’d git so sulky en stubborn dat de w’ite folks couldn’ ha’dly do nuffin wid ’im.

===Chinese===
「不肯，」我答道，「我看你很难让我们相信这种事。」

「哎呀，朱利叶斯大叔！」安妮正色道，「多么可笑的胡话！」

老人的话落得这般对待，顿时哑口无言；我费了好一番外交手腕，才劝得他肯赐予一番解释。眼看一个漫长而乏味的下午横在面前，实在没什么吸引力，能有一段种植园传说来解一解安息日的沉闷，我正求之不得。

「俺年轻那会儿，」朱利叶斯终于被我们劝动，讲起故事来，「那个拐脚的黑人——他名叫普里默斯（Primus）——从前往在伦伯顿木板路那头的老吉姆·麦吉（Jim McGee）老爷家。俺那时候常上那边去看住在庄上的一个女人；这些事俺就是这样得知的。这位普里默斯是庄上最活泛的劳力，成天又跳舞，又喝酒，又到处串门子，又唱歌，又拨班卓琴；只是隔三差五，赶上他觉得什么事上头没受着公道对待，就会闷头犟脑，闹得白人们几乎拿他没办法。

===Original===
“It wuz ’gin’ de rules fer any er de han’s ter go ’way fum de plantation at night; but Primus didn’ min’ de rules, en went w’en he felt lack it; en de w’ite folks purten’ lack dey didn’ know it, fer Primus was dange’ous w’en he got in dem stubborn spells, en dey’d ruther not fool wid ’im.

===Chinese===
「庄上的规矩，不许任何劳力夜里离开种植园；可普里默斯没把规矩放在心上，想去就去；白人们也只装作不知，因为普里默斯犯了那股犟劲的时候是危险的，他们宁可不去招惹他。

===Original===
“One night in de spring er de year, Primus slip’ off fum de plantation, en went down on de Wim’l’ton Road ter a dance gun by some er de free niggers down dere. Dey wuz a fiddle, en a banjo, en a jug gwine roun’ on de outside, en Primus sung en dance’ ’tel ’long ’bout two o’clock in de mawnin’, w’en he start’ fer home. Ez he come erlong back, he tuk a nigh-cut ’cross de cottonfiel’s en ’long by de aidge er de Min’al Spring Swamp, so ez ter git shet er de patteroles w’at rid up en down de big road fer ter keep de darkies fum runnin’ roun’ nights. Primus was sa’nt’rin’ ’long, studyin’ ’bout de good time he’d had wid de gals, w’en, ez he wuz gwine by a fence co’nder, w’at sh’d he heah but sump’n grunt. He stopped a minute ter listen, en he heared sump’n grunt ag’in. Den he went ober ter de fence whar he heard de fuss, en dere, layin’ in de fence co’nder, on a pile er pine straw, he seed a fine, fat shote.

===Chinese===
「那年春天的一夜，普里默斯溜出种植园，下到威明顿大道，去参加那边自由黑人办的一场舞会。外场上一把提琴、一只班卓琴，还有一坛子酒轮着传，普里默斯又唱又跳，直闹到凌晨两点来钟才动身回家。回来的路上，他抄近道穿过一片片棉田，沿着矿泉沼泽（Mineral Spring Swamp）边上走，好躲开那些在大路上来回巡行、专防黑人夜里乱跑的巡逻队（patteroles）。普里默斯正信步溜达着，回味同姑娘们厮混的那段好时光，走过一个栅栏拐角，你道他听见了什么——有什么东西哼了一声。他站住听了听，又听得一声哼。他便走到传出动静的那截栅栏跟前，只见栅栏拐角里、一堆松针上，卧着一头肥壮体面的猪崽（shote）。

===Original===
“Primus look’ ha’d at de shote, en den sta’ted home. But somehow er ’nudder he couldn’ git away fum dat shote; w’en he tuk one step for’ards wid one foot, de yuther foot ’peared ter take two steps back’ards, en so he kep’ nachly gittin’ closeter en closeter ter de shote. It was de beatin’es’ thing! De shote des ’peared ter cha’m Primus, en fus’ thing you know Primus foun’ hisse’f ’way up de road wid de shote on his back.

===Chinese===
「普里默斯把那猪崽端详了好一阵，随后抬脚回家。可是不知怎么的，他就是离不开那猪崽；他一只脚朝前迈一步，另一只脚倒像朝后退了两步，于是他自然而然地越挨越近。这真是天下头一份的稀罕事！那猪崽简直像给普里默斯施了迷魂法，说时迟那时快，普里默斯一明白过来，自己已经到了大路上老远的一段，背上驮着那头猪崽。

===Original===
“Ef Primus had ’a’ knowed whose shote dat wuz, he’d ’a’ manage’ ter git pas’ it somehow er ’nudder. Ez it happen’, de shote b’long ter a cunjuh man w’at libbed down in de free-nigger sett’ement. Co’se de cunjuh man didn’ hab ter wuk his roots but a little w’ile ’fo’ he foun’ out who tuk his shote, en den de trouble begun. One mawnin’, a day er so later, en befo’ he got de shote eat up, Primus didn’ go ter wuk w’en de hawn blow, en w’en de oberseah wen’ ter look fer him, dey wa’ no trace er Primus ter be ’skivered nowhar. W’en he didn’ come back in a day er so mo’, eve’ybody on de plantation ’lowed he had runned erway. His marster a’vertise’ him in de papers, en offered a big reward fer ’im. De nigger-ketchers fotch out dey dogs, en track’ ’im down ter de aidge er de swamp, en den de scent gun out; en dat was de las’ anybody seed er Primus fer a long, long time.

===Chinese===
「普里默斯要是知道那猪崽是谁家的，怎么着也会想个法子绕开它。偏偏不巧，那猪崽的主人，是住在下头自由黑人聚居区的一个巫师（cunjuh man）。自然，巫师只消摆弄一小会儿咒根，就查明了是谁拿走他的猪崽，麻烦跟着就来了。过了一两天的一个早晨——那头猪崽还没吃完呢——吹号上工的时候，普里默斯没出工；监工去找他，哪儿也寻不见普里默斯的一点踪影。又过了一两天他还不见回来，庄园上人人断定他是跑了。他的主人在报上登了启事，悬出一大笔赏格捉他。捕奴人放出狗来，一路追到沼泽边上，气味断了；打那以后，很长很长的日子，谁也没再见过普里默斯的影子。

===Original===
“Two er th’ee weeks atter Primus disappear’, his marster went ter town one Sad’day. Mars Jim was stan’in’ in front er Sandy Campbell’s bar-room, up by de ole wagon-ya’d, w’en a po’ w’ite man fum down on de Wim’l’ton Road come up ter ’im en ax’ ’im, kinder keerless lack, ef he didn’ wanter buy a mule.

===Chinese===
「普里默斯失踪后过了两三个礼拜，他的主人有一个礼拜六进了城。吉姆老爷正站在老车马场旁边桑迪·坎贝尔（Sandy Campbell）的酒馆门前，一个从威明顿大道那头来的穷白人凑上前来，装出副满不在乎的架势，问他想不想买头骡子。

===Original===
“ ‘I dunno,’ says Mars Jim; ‘it ’pen’s on de mule, en on de price. Whar is de mule?’

“ ‘Des ’roun’ heah back er ole Tom McAllister’s sto’,’ says de po’ w’ite man.

“ ‘I reckon I’ll hab a look at de mule,’ says Mars Jim, ‘en ef he suit me, I dunno but w’at I mought buy ’im.’

===Chinese===
「『说不好，』吉姆老爷道，『得看骡子怎么样，也得看价钱。骡子在哪儿？』

「『就在这儿，老汤姆·麦卡利斯特（Tom McAllister）的铺子后头。』穷白人道。

「『我看就去瞧上一眼，』吉姆老爷道，『要是中意，倒也不妨买下它。』

===Original===
“So de po’ w’ite man tuk Mars Jim ’roun’ back er de sto’, en dere stood a monst’us fine mule. W’en de mule see Mars Jim, he gun a whinny, des lack he knowed him befo’. Mars Jim look’ at de mule, en de mule ’peared ter be soun’ en strong. Mars Jim ’lowed dey ’peared ter be sump’n fermilyus ’bout de mule’s face, ’spesh’ly his eyes; but he hadn’ los’ naer mule, en didn’ hab no recommemb’ance er habin’ seed de mule befo’. He ax’ de po’ buckrah whar he got de mule, en de po’ buckrah say his brer raise’ de mule down on Rockfish Creek. Mars Jim was a little s’picious er seein’ a po’ w’ite man wid sech a fine creetur, but he fin’lly ’greed ter gib de man fifty dollars fer de mule﻿—’bout ha’f w’at a good mule was wuf dem days.

===Chinese===
「于是穷白人领着吉姆老爷绕到铺子后头，那里立着一头顶顶体面的骡子。骡子一见吉姆老爷，就嘶叫了一声，像是早就认得他似的。吉姆老爷端详那骡子，只见它筋强骨壮、无病无伤。吉姆老爷只觉这骡子的脸盘，尤其那双眼睛，似乎有点眼熟；可他没丢过骡子，也不记得从前见过这头骡子。他问那穷白人（buckrah）骡子是哪儿来的，穷白人说，骡子是他兄弟在罗克菲什溪（Rockfish Creek）那边喂大的。眼见一个穷白人手里攥着这么一头好牲口，吉姆老爷心里不免犯点嘀咕，但末了还是讲定，给那人五十块钱买下这骡子——大约只抵当年一头好骡子身价的一半。

===Original===
“He tied de mule behin’ de buggy w’en he went home, en put ’im ter ploughin’ cotton de nex’ day. De mule done mighty well fer th’ee er fo’ days, en den de niggers ’mence’ ter notice some quare things erbout him. Dey wuz a medder on de plantation whar dey use’ ter put de hosses en mules ter pastur’. Hit was fence’ off fum de corn-fiel’ on one side, but on de yuther side’n de pastur’ was a terbacker-patch w’at wa’n’t fence’ off, ’ca’se de beastisses doan none un ’em eat terbacker. Dey doan know w’at’s good! Terbacker is lack religion, de good Lawd made it fer people, en dey ain’ no yuther creetur w’at kin ’preciate it. De darkies notice’ dat de fus’ thing de new mule done, w’en he was turnt inter de pastur’, wuz ter make fer de terbacker-patch. Co’se dey didn’ think nuffin un it, but nex’ mawnin’, w’en dey went ter ketch ’im, dey ’skivered dat he had eat up two whole rows er terbacker plants. Atter dat dey had ter put a halter on ’im, en tie ’im ter a stake, er e’se dey wouldn’ ’a’ been naer leaf er terbacker lef’ in de patch.

===Chinese===
「回家时他把骡子拴在马车后头带回去，第二天便套上它犁棉田。头三四天，骡子干得挺像样，随后黑人们就开始觉察出它身上有些古怪。庄园上有一片草地，惯常把马和骡子赶进去放牧。草地一侧同玉米田隔着栅栏，可草地另一头是一片烟叶地，没有拦起来，因为牲口们谁也不吃烟叶。它们不懂得什么才算好东西！烟叶就像宗教一样，是好上帝造给人享用的，再没有别的牲口识得赏识它了。黑人们注意到，这头新骡子一放进草地，头一件事就是直奔烟叶地去。他们当然没把这放在心上，可第二天早上去牵它时，才发现它把整整两垄烟苗都吃了个精光。打那以后，他们只好给它套上笼头，拴在木桩上，要不然那片烟叶地只怕连一片叶子也剩不下来。

===Original===
“Ernudder day one er de han’s, name’ ’Dolphus, hitch’ de mule up, en dribe up here ter dis yer vimya’d﻿—dat wuz w’en ole Mars Dugal’ own’ dis place. Mars Dugal’ had kilt a yearlin’, en de naber w’ite folks all sont ober fer ter git some fraish beef, en Mars Jim had sont ’Dolphus fer some too. Dey wuz a winepress in de ya’d whar ’Dolphus lef’ de mule a-stan’in’, en right in front er de press dey wuz a tub er grape-juice, des pressed out, en a little ter one side a bairl erbout half full er wine w’at had be’n stan’in’ two er th’ee days, en had begun ter git sorter sha’p ter de tas’e. Dey wuz a couple er bo’ds on top er dis yer bairl, wid a rock laid on ’em ter hol’ ’em down. Ez I wuz a-sayin’, ’Dolphus lef’ de mule stan’in’ in de ya’d, en went inter de smoke-house fer ter git de beef. Bimeby, w’en he come out, he seed de mule a-stagg’rin’ ’bout de ya’d; en ’fo’ ’Dolphus could git dere ter fin’ out w’at wuz de matter, de mule fell right ober on his side, en laid dere des’ lack he was dead.

===Chinese===
「又有一天，庄上一个名叫多尔弗斯（'Dolphus）的劳力套上这骡子，赶车上这边来，到如今这处葡萄园——那还是老杜加尔老爷置着这份产业的时候。杜加尔老爷宰了一头当年生的小牛，邻近的白人人家都打发人来讨些鲜牛肉，吉姆老爷也差多尔弗斯来讨一份。院里有一座榨酒架，多尔弗斯把骡子拴在架子旁边；榨架正前方摆着一桶才榨出来的葡萄汁，略偏一点还有一只木桶，装着约莫半桶葡萄酒，搁了两三天，味道已经开始发冲了。桶口搭着两块木板，板上压着块石头镇着。俺方才说了，多尔弗斯把骡子拴在院里，便进熏肉房取牛肉去了。过了半晌他出来，只见那骡子正在院子里东倒西歪地打晃；不等多尔弗斯赶过去瞧瞧是怎么回事，骡子扑通一声侧身倒下，直挺挺躺在那儿，就跟死了一样。

===Original===
“All de niggers ’bout de house run out dere fer ter see w’at wuz de matter. Some say de mule had de colic; some say one thing en some ernudder; ’tel bimeby one er de han’s seed de top wuz off’n de bairl, en run en looked in.

“ ‘Fo’ de Lawd!’ he say, ‘dat mule drunk! he be’n drinkin’ de wine.’ En sho’ ’nuff, de mule had pas’ right by de tub er fraish grape-juice en push’ de kiver off’n de bairl, en drunk two er th’ee gallon er de wine w’at had been stan’in’ long ernough fer ter begin ter git sha’p.

===Chinese===
「屋前屋后的黑人全跑到那儿去看出了什么事。有的说骡子得了绞痛，有的说这、有的说那；直到后来，有个劳力看见木桶的盖板掀在一旁，跑过去往桶里一瞧。

「『老天爷在上！』他嚷道，『这骡子醉了！它把酒喝了。』果然不差：那骡子分明是从那桶鲜葡萄汁旁边径直走过，拱开桶上的盖板，把那桶搁得时日够久、味道已经发冲的酒，喝掉了两三加仑。

===Original===
“De darkies all made a great ’miration ’bout de mule gittin’ drunk. Dey never hadn’ seed nuffin lack it in dey bawn days. Dey po’d water ober de mule, en tried ter sober ’im up; but it wa’n’t no use, en ’Dolphus had ter take de beef home on his back, en leabe de mule dere, ’tel he slep’ off ’is spree.

===Chinese===
「骡子喝醉这件事，黑人们个个啧啧称奇。活了这一辈子，他们谁也没见过这样的新鲜事。他们往骡子身上一桶桶浇凉水，想叫它醒醒酒；可全不管用，多尔弗斯只好把牛肉背回家去，把骡子撂在那儿，由它睡醒这一场酒去。

===Original===
“I doan ’member whe’r I tol’ you er no, but w’en Primus disappear’ fum de plantation, he lef’ a wife behin’ ’im﻿—a monst’us good-lookin’ yaller gal, name’ Sally. W’en Primus had be’n gone a mont’ er so, Sally ’mence’ fer ter git lonesome, en tuk up wid ernudder young man name’ Dan, w’at b’long’ on de same plantation. One day dis yer Dan tuk de noo mule out in de cotton-fiel’ fer ter plough, en w’en dey wuz gwine ’long de tu’n-row, who sh’d he meet but dis yer Sally. Dan look’ ’roun’ en he didn’ see de oberseah nowhar, so he stop’ a minute fer ter run on wid Sally.

===Chinese===
「俺记不得跟您说过没有——普里默斯从庄园上失踪的时候，撇下了一个媳妇——一个模样顶标致的黄皮肤姑娘，名叫萨莉（Sally）。普里默斯走了个把月，萨莉渐渐耐不住冷清，跟另一个年轻人好上了，那年轻人名叫丹（Dan），也是这庄园上的人。有一天，这个丹牵着新骡子下棉田犁地，顺着地头的拐垄往前走，迎面碰上的不是别人，正是萨莉。丹四下里一望，不见监工的影子，便停下来，跟萨莉搭着话调笑。

===Original===
“ ‘Hoddy, honey,’ sezee. ‘How you feelin’ dis mawnin’?’

“ ‘Fus’ rate,’ ’spon’ Sally.

“Dey wuz lookin’ at one ernudder, en dey didn’ naer one un ’em pay no ’tention ter de mule, who had turnt ’is head ’roun’ en wuz lookin’ at Sally ez ha’d ez he could, en stretchin’ ’is neck en raisin’ ’is years, en whinnyin’ kinder sof’ ter hisse’f.

===Chinese===
「『你好哇，心肝，』他道，『今儿早上觉得怎么样？』

「『好得很。』萨莉应道。

「两人你瞧着我、我瞧着你，谁也没把那骡子放在心上；骡子却早已扭过头来，使足了劲盯着萨莉瞧，又伸长脖子，支棱起耳朵，独自个儿轻轻地打着嘶儿。

===Original===
“ ‘Yas, honey,’ ’lows Dan, ‘en you gwine ter feel fus’ rate long ez you sticks ter me. Fer I’s a better man dan dat low-down runaway nigger Primus dat you be’n wastin’ yo’ time wid.’

===Chinese===
「『是喽，心肝，』丹道，『只要你跟定了俺，往后天天都好得很。俺可比那个下三滥的逃奴普里默斯强多了，你跟他混日子，纯粹是白耽误工夫。』

===Original===
“Dan had let go de plough-handle, en had put his arm ’roun’ Sally, en wuz des gwine ter kiss her, w’en sump’n ketch’ ’im by de scruff er de neck en flung ’im ’way ober in de cotton-patch. W’en he pick’ ’isse’f up, Sally had gone kitin’ down de tu’n-row, en de mule wuz stan’in’ dere lookin’ ez ca’m en peaceful ez a Sunday mawnin’.

===Chinese===
「丹已经撒开犁把，一条胳膊搂住萨莉，正要亲她，忽有什么东西一把攥住他的后颈，把他整个儿甩出去，摔在棉花地里老远。等他爬起身来，萨莉早已顺着垄沟一溜烟跑没了影，那骡子站在原地，神情安详太平，活像礼拜天清晨一样。

===Original===
“Fus’ Dan had ’lowed it wuz de oberseah w’at had cotch’ ’im wastin’ ’is time. But dey wa’n’t no oberseah in sight, so he ’cluded it must ’a’ be’n de mule. So he pitch’ inter de mule en lammed ’im ez ha’d ez he could. De mule tuk it all, en ’peared ter be ez ’umble ez a mule could be; but w’en dey wuz makin’ de turn at de een’ er de row, one er de plough-lines got under de mule’s hin’ leg. Dan retch’ down ter git de line out, sorter keerless like, w’en de mule haul’ off en kick him clean ober de fence inter a brier-patch on de yuther side.

===Chinese===
「起初丹还当是监工逮住了他磨洋工。可四下里不见监工的影子，他断定必是那骡子干的好事。于是他扑上那骡子，使出全身的劲儿狠狠抽它。骡子一声不吭全受了，显出一副骡子所能有的低眉顺眼；可是到垄头拐弯的时候，一条犁绳滑到了骡子的后腿底下。丹漫不经心地弯腰去掏那条绳，骡子却撩起后腿，一脚把他踢过栅栏，端端正正落在那一边的荆棘丛里。

===Original===
“Dan wuz mighty so’ fum ’is woun’s en scratches, en wuz laid up fer two er th’ee days. One night de noo mule got out’n de pastur’, en went down to de quarters. Dan wuz layin’ dere on his pallet, w’en he heard sump’n bangin’ erway at de side er his cabin. He raise’ up on one shoulder en look’ roun’, w’en w’at should he see but de noo mule’s head stickin’ in de winder, wid his lips drawed back over his toofs, grinnin’ en snappin’ at Dan des’ lack he wanter eat ’im up. Den de mule went roun’ ter de do’, en kick’ erway lack he wanter break de do’ down, ’tel bimeby somebody come ’long en driv him back ter de pastur’. W’en Sally come in a little later fum de big house, whar she’d be’n waitin’ on de w’ite folks, she foun’ po’ Dan nigh ’bout dead, he wuz so skeered. She ’lowed Dan had had de nightmare; but w’en dey look’ at de do’, dey seed de marks er de mule’s huffs, so dey couldn’ be no mistake ’bout w’at had happen’.

===Chinese===
「丹叫伤处和划破的口子折磨得够呛，在铺上躺了两三天。一天夜里，新骡子逃出草地，下到黑奴住区去了。丹正躺在草垫上，忽听得什么东西在他小屋的板壁上咚咚地撞。他撑起一边肩膀四下一望，你道他看见什么——新骡子的脑袋正从窗户里探进来，嘴咧着、牙龇着，冲着丹又笑又咬，活像要一口把他吞下肚去。随后骡子绕到门口，一路踢腾，像要把门踢塌，直到后来有人打那儿经过，才把它赶回草地去了。过了一会儿，萨莉从大宅伺候完白人回来，发现可怜的丹吓得半死。她琢磨着丹是魇着了、做了噩梦；可再看看那扇门，上头清清楚楚印着骡蹄的印子，出了什么事，再没有半点疑问了。

===Original===
“Co’se de niggers tol’ dey marster ’bout de mule’s gwines-on. Fust he didn’ pay no ’tention ter it, but atter a w’ile he tol’ ’em ef dey didn’ stop dey foolis’ness, he gwine tie some un ’em up. So atter dat dey didn’ say nuffin mo’ ter dey marster, but dey kep’ on noticin’ de mule’s quare ways des de same.

===Chinese===
「黑人们自然把这些骡子的行径禀告了主人。起初他全不当回事，后来便放了话：他们要是再不住嘴胡闹，就要捆起他们中间的一两个来抽。打那以后，他们不再向主人提起一个字，可照样暗暗留意着骡子的古怪行径。

===Original===
“ ’Long ’bout de middle er de summer dey wuz a big camp-meetin’ broke out down on de Wim’l’ton Road, en nigh ’bout all de po’ w’ite folks en free niggers in de settlement got ’ligion, en lo en behol’! ’mongs’ ’em wuz de cunjuh man w’at own’ de shote w’at cha’med Primus.

===Chinese===
「到了仲夏前后，威明顿大道那头兴起了一场大野营布道会（camp-meetin'），那一带的穷白人和自由黑人，差不多全都信了教。你瞧怎么着！其中就有那位巫师——就是那头迷住普里默斯的猪崽的主人。

===Original===
“Dis cunjuh man wuz a Guinea nigger, en befo’ he wuz sot free had use’ ter b’long ter a gent’eman down in Sampson County. De cunjuh man say his daddy wuz a king, er a guv’ner, er some sorter w’at-you-may-call-’em ’way ober yander in Affiky whar de niggers come fum, befo’ he was stoled erway en sol’ ter de spekilaters. De cunjuh man had he’ped his marster out’n some trouble ernudder wid his goopher, en his marster had sot him free, en bought him a trac’ er land down on de Wim’l’ton Road. He purten’ ter be a cow-doctor, but eve’ybody knowed w’at he r’al’y wuz.

===Chinese===
「这位巫师是个几内亚（Guinea）黑人，没被放免之前，原属于下头桑普森县（Sampson County）的一位绅士。据巫师自己说，他爹在老远的非洲——黑人的老家——做过国王，要不就是总督，反正是个名目说不清的大人物；后来他自己叫人拐了去，卖给人贩子（spekilater）。这位巫师曾用咒（goopher）替他主人解过什么难处，主人便放他自由，又在威明顿大道下头给他买了一块地。他对外只装作个牛医，可谁都明白他真正是干什么的。

===Original===
“De cunjuh man hadn’ mo’ d’n come th’oo good, befo’ he wuz tuk sick wid a col’ w’at he kotch kneelin’ on de groun’ so long at de mou’ners’ bench. He kep’ gittin’ wusser en wusser, en bimeby de rheumatiz tuk holt er ’im, en drawed him all up, ’tel one day he sont word up ter Mars Jim McGee’s plantation, en ax’ Pete, de nigger w’at tuk keer er de mules, fer ter come down dere dat night en fetch dat mule w’at his marster had bought fum de po’ w’ite man dyoin’ er de summer.

===Chinese===
「巫师的好日子还没过几天，就病倒了，得的是风寒——因为在悔罪凳前跪得太久，跪出来的。他的病一天重似一天，不久风湿又缠上身，把他整个人抽缩作一团；终于有一天，他捎话到吉姆·麦吉老爷的庄园上，请求皮特（Pete）——就是照料骡马的那个黑人——当天夜里下他那儿去一趟，把他主人夏天从穷白人手里买来的那头骡子也一并牵来。

===Original===
“Pete didn’ know w’at de cunjuh man wuz dribin’ at, but he didn’ daster stay way; en so dat night, w’en he’d done eat his bacon en his hoe-cake, en drunk his ’lasses-en-water, he put a bridle on de mule, en rid ’im down ter de cunjuh man’s cabin. W’en he got ter de do’, he lit en hitch’ de mule, en den knock’ at de do’. He felt mighty jubous ’bout gwine in, but he was bleedst ter do it; he knowed he couldn’ he’p ’isse’f.

===Chinese===
「皮特猜不透巫师打的什么主意，可又不敢不去；于是那天夜里，他吃罢咸肉和玉米烤饼，喝完糖蜜水，给骡子套上笼头，骑着下到巫师的小屋去。到了门口，他翻身下来，拴好骡子，叩了叩门。要进这道门，他心里直打鼓，可他非进不可；他知道自己是身不由己。

===Original===
“ ‘Pull de string,’ sez a weak voice, en w’en Pete lif’ de latch en went in, de cunjuh man was layin’ on de bed, lookin’ pale en weak, lack he didn’ hab much longer fer ter lib.

“ ‘Is you fotch’ de mule?’ sezee.

“Pete say yas, en de cunjuh man kep’ on.

===Chinese===
「『拉门绳吧。』一个虚弱的声音说道。皮特提起门闩走进去，只见巫师躺在床上，脸色苍白，气息微弱，看样子在世上撑不了多久了。

「『骡子你牵来了么？』巫师问。

「皮特说来啦，巫师便接着往下说。

===Original===
“ ‘Brer Pete,’ sezee, ‘I’s be’n a monst’us sinner man, en I’s done a power er wickedness endyoin’ er my days; but de good Lawd is wash’ my sins erway, en I feels now dat I’s boun’ fer de kingdom. En I feels, too, dat I ain’ gwine ter git up fum dis bed no mo’ in dis worl’, en I wants ter ondo some er de harm I done. En dat’s de reason, Brer Pete, I sont fer you ter fetch dat mule down here. You ’member dat shote I was up ter yo’ plantation inquirin’ ’bout las’ June?’

===Chinese===
「『皮特弟兄，』他道，『俺这辈子是个作孽顶重的人，一辈子干下的坏事数也数不清；可好上帝已经把俺的罪洗净了，俺如今觉得，天国是俺注定要去的地方。俺还觉得，俺在这世上再也起不了这张床了，俺想趁此把俺造下的孽补救几分。皮特弟兄，俺就是为了这个，才打发你去把骡子牵下来。你还记得去年六月，俺上你们庄园打听过的那头猪崽么？』

===Original===
“ ‘Yas,’ says Brer Pete, ‘I ’member yo’ axin’ ’bout a shote you had los’.’

===Chinese===
「『记得，』皮特弟兄道，『记得你问起过你丢的一头猪崽。』

===Original===
“ ‘I dunno whe’r you eber l’arnt it er no,’ says de cunjuh man, ‘but I done knowed yo’ marster’s Primus had tuk de shote, en I wuz boun’ ter git eben wid ’im. So one night I cotch’ ’im down by de swamp on his way ter a candy-pullin’, en I th’owed a goopher mixtry on ’im, en turnt ’im ter a mule, en got a po’ w’ite man ter sell de mule, en we ’vided de money. But I doan want ter die ’tel I turn Brer Primus back ag’in.’

===Chinese===
「『这事你们后来听说了没有，俺不知道，』巫师道，『可俺早就查明，是你们主人家的普里默斯偷了那头猪崽，俺打定主意，非跟他清算这笔账不可。于是有一夜，他在沼泽边上去赴拉糖会（candy-pullin'）的当口，俺逮住了他，给他施了一道咒药（goopher mixtry），把他变成一头骡子，又寻了个穷白人把这骡子卖掉，卖的钱俺们俩分了。可俺要是不先把普里默斯弟兄变回原样，俺死也不甘心。』

===Original===
“Den de cunjuh man ax’ Pete ter take down one er two go’ds off’n a she’f in de corner, en one er two bottles wid some kin’ er mixtry in ’em, en set ’em on a stool by de bed; en den he ax’ ’im ter fetch de mule in.

“W’en de mule come in de do’, he gin a snort, en started fer de bed, des lack he was gwine ter jump on it.

===Chinese===
「随后巫师请皮特从墙角的架子上取下一两只葫芦瓢（gourd），还有一两瓶盛着什么药水的瓶子，摆到床边的凳子上；接着又叫他牵骡子进来。

「骡子进门时打了个响鼻，直朝床上冲去，像要一跃扑上床来。

===Original===
“ ‘Hol’ on dere, Brer Primus!’ de cunjuh man hollered. ‘I’s monst’us weak, en ef you ’mence on me, you won’t nebber hab no chance fer ter git turn’ back no mo’.’

===Chinese===
「『且慢，普里默斯弟兄！』巫师喊道，『俺虚弱得很，你要是扑上来，你就再也没机会变回人身了。』

===Original===
“De mule seed de sense er dat, en stood still. Den de cunjuh man tuk de go’ds en bottles, en ’mence’ ter wuk de roots en yarbs, en de mule ’mence’ ter turn back ter a man﻿—fust his years, den de res’ er his head, den his shoulders en arms. All de time de cunjuh man kep’ on wukkin’ his roots; en Pete en Primus could see he wuz gittin’ weaker en weaker all de time.

===Chinese===
「骡子听出这话在理，便站住不动了。于是巫师拿起葫芦瓢和瓶子，动手施展起咒根草药来，骡子便开始变回人形——先是耳朵，再是头的其余部分，然后是肩膀和手臂。巫师一直不停地施着咒根；皮特和普里默斯都看得出，他的气力一刻比一刻衰弱下去。

===Original===
“ ‘Brer Pete,’ sezee, bimeby, ‘gimme a drink er dem bitters out’n dat green bottle on de she’f yander. I’s gwine fas’, en it’ll gimme strenk fer ter finish dis wuk.’

===Chinese===
「『皮特弟兄，』过了半晌，他又道，『把那边架子上的绿瓶里那点药酒，倒一口给俺。俺快撑不住了，喝一口，俺才有力气把这桩事做完。』

===Original===
“Brer Pete look’ up on de mantelpiece, en he seed a bottle in de corner. It was so da’k in de cabin he couldn’ tell whe’r it wuz a green bottle er no. But he hilt de bottle ter de cunjuh man’s mouf, en he tuk a big mouff’l. He hadn’ mo’ d’n swallowed it befo’ he ’mence’ ter holler.

“ ‘You gimme de wrong bottle, Brer Pete; dis yer bottle’s got pizen in it, en I’s done fer dis time, sho’. Hol’ me up, fer de Lawd’s sake! ’tel I git th’oo turnin’ Brer Primus back.’

===Chinese===
「皮特弟兄朝壁炉架上一望，看见角落里有只瓶子。屋里太黑，他辨不出那是不是只绿瓶。可他还是把瓶子凑到巫师嘴边，巫师大大地灌了一口。这一口才咽下去，他就嚷起来了。

「『你拿错瓶子了，皮特弟兄；这瓶里是毒药，这一回俺是完了，没跑了。看在上帝分上，扶俺坐起来！让俺撑到把普里默斯弟兄变完。』

===Original===
“So Pete hilt him up, en he kep’ on wukkin’ de roots, ’tel he got de goopher all tuk off’n Brer Primus ’cep’n’ one foot. He hadn’ got dis foot mo’ d’n half turnt back befo’ his strenk gun out enti’ely, en he drap’ de roots en fell back on de bed.

===Chinese===
「于是皮特扶着他，他接着施展咒根，直到把普里默斯弟兄身上的咒统统解尽，只剩下一只脚。那只脚刚变回来一半，他的气力便完全使尽了，咒根脱了手，人向后倒在床上。

===Original===
“ ‘I can’t do no mo’ fer you, Brer Primus,’ sezee, ‘but I hopes you will fergib me fer w’at harm I done you. I knows de good Lawd done fergib me, en I hope ter meet you bofe in glory. I sees de good angels waitin’ fer me up yander, wid a long w’ite robe en a starry crown, en I’m on my way ter jine ’em.’ En so de cunjuh man died, en Pete en Primus went back ter de plantation.

===Chinese===
「『普里默斯弟兄，俺再也帮不了你了，』他说，『可俺盼你饶恕俺加害你的罪过。俺知道好上帝已经饶恕了俺，俺盼着在荣耀里，同你们两位相会。俺看见善良的天使们在上面等着俺呢，穿着雪白的长袍，戴着缀星的冠冕，俺这就动身去同他们会合。』巫师就这样死了，皮特和普里默斯回庄园去了。

===Original===
“De darkies all made a great ’miration w’en Primus come back. Mars Jim let on lack he didn’ b’lieve de tale de two niggers tol’; he sez Primus had runned erway, en stay’ ’tel he got ti’ed er de swamps, en den come back on him ter be fed. He tried ter ’count fer de shape er Primus’ foot by sayin’ Primus got his foot smash’, er snake-bit, er sump’n, w’iles he wuz erway, en den stayed out in de woods whar he couldn’ git it kyoed up straight, ’stidder comin’ long home whar a doctor could ’a’ ’tended ter it. But de niggers all notice’ dey marster didn’ tie Primus up, ner take on much ’ca’se de mule wuz gone. So dey ’lowed dey marster must ’a’ had his s’picions ’bout dat cunjuh man.”

===Chinese===
「普里默斯回来时，黑人们个个啧啧称奇。吉姆老爷只装作不信那两个黑人讲的一套；他说普里默斯是自己跑了，在外头待到腻烦了沼泽地，又跑回来吃他家的饭。至于普里默斯那只脚的形状，他解释说，普里默斯在外头那阵子把脚砸了，要不就是叫蛇咬了，反正伤了点什么，又赖在林子里不回家，没法把脚治端正，倒不肯早些回来，好让医生诊治。可黑人们都留意到，主人既没有捆起普里默斯来抽，骡子丢了也没怎么动气。于是他们琢磨，主人对那位巫师，只怕心里早就有几分数了。」

===Original===
My wife had listened to Julius’s recital with only a mild interest. When the old man had finished it she remarked:﻿—

“That story does not appeal to me, Uncle Julius, and is not up to your usual mark. It isn’t pathetic, it has no moral that I can discover, and I can’t see why you should tell it. In fact, it seems to me like nonsense.”

The old man looked puzzled as well as pained. He had not pleased the lady, and he did not seem to understand why.

===Chinese===
我妻子听朱利叶斯这一番讲述，兴致只算平平。等老人讲完，她开口道——

「这个故事打动不了我，朱利叶斯大叔，也够不上你往常的水准。它不凄婉动人，也看不出有什么寓意，我不明白你为什么要讲它。说实在的，我看它简直就是无稽之谈。」

老人一脸困惑，又一脸委屈。他没能讨这位太太的欢心，而他似乎又不明白缘由何在。

===Original===
“I’m sorry, ma’m,” he said reproachfully, “ef you doan lack dat tale. I can’t make out w’at you means by some er dem wo’ds you uses, but I’m tellin’ nuffin but de truf. Co’se I didn’ see de cunjuh man tu’n ’im back, fer I wuzn’ dere; but I be’n hearin’ de tale fer twenty-five yeahs, en I ain’ got no ’casion fer ter ’spute it. Dey’s so many things a body knows is lies, dat dey ain’ no use gwine roun’ findin’ fault wid tales dat mought des ez well be so ez not. F’ instance, dey’s a young nigger gwine ter school in town, en he come out heah de yuther day en ’lowed dat de sun stood still en de yeath turnt roun’ eve’y day on a kinder axletree. I tol’ dat young nigger ef he didn’ take hisse’f ’way wid dem lies, I’d take a buggy-trace ter ’im; fer I sees de yeath stan’in’ still all de time, en I sees de sun gwine roun’ it, en ef a man can’t b’lieve w’at ’e sees, I can’t see no use in libbin’﻿—mought ’s well die en be whar we can’t see nuffin. En ernudder thing w’at proves de tale ’bout dis ole Primus is de way he goes on ef anybody ax’ him how he come by dat club-foot. I axed ’im one day, mighty perlite en civil, en he call’ me a’ ole fool, en got so mad he ain’ spoke ter me sence. Hit’s monst’us quare. But dis is a quare worl’, anyway yer kin fix it,” concluded the old man, with a weary sigh.

===Chinese===
「您要是不喜欢那个故事，俺心里可过意不去，太太，」他带着几分责怪的口气说。「您用的有些字眼，俺实在琢磨不透是什么意思，可俺讲的句句是实话。巫师把他变回来那一节，俺自然没亲眼见过，因为俺不在场；可这故事俺听了有二十五年了，俺没有一点因由去疑它。人明知是谎的事情已经多得数不清，犯不上再到处挑刺，跟那些说不准是真是假的故事过不去。打个比方，镇上有个上学的年轻黑人，前几天跑到这儿来，说是什么太阳待着不动，倒是大地天天围着一根车轴似的玩意儿转。俺对那年轻黑人说，他要是再夹带这些鬼话上门，俺就拿马车挽绳抽他；因为俺明明成天看见大地待着不动，看见太阳围着它转，一个人要是连自己亲眼见的都信不得，那活着还有什么意思——倒不如死了干净，落个什么都瞧不见。还有一件事，也能证明这位老普里默斯的故事不假：谁要是问他那只拐脚是怎么得下的，就瞧他那个闹腾劲儿。有一天俺客客气气、恭恭敬敬地问了他一句，他骂俺老糊涂，气得打那以后，再没跟俺说过一句话。真是邪门得很。不过话说回来，这本来就是个古怪的世道，随您怎么摆布它，它都是那么古怪。」老人末了乏乏地叹了口气，下了结论。

===Original===
“Ef you makes up yo’ min’ not ter buy dat mule, suh,” he added, as he rose to go, “I knows a man w’at’s got a good hoss he wants ter sell﻿—leas’ways dat’s w’at I heared. I’m gwine ter pra’rmeetin’ ter-night, en I’m gwine right by de man’s house, en ef you’d lack ter look at de hoss, I’ll ax ’im ter fetch him roun’.”

“Oh, yes,” I said, “you can ask him to stop in, if he is passing. There will be no harm in looking at the horse, though I rather think I shall buy a mule.”

===Chinese===
「您要是打定了主意不买骡子，先生，」他起身要走时，又添了一句，「俺认得一个人，手里有匹好马要出手——反正俺是听人这么说的。俺今儿夜里要去祷告会（pra'rmeetin'），正好打那人家门前过，您要是乐意瞧瞧那匹马，俺就叫他牵过来。」

「哦，行，」我说，「他要是路过，你可以请他进来坐坐。看看那匹马没什么坏处，不过我多半还是买骡子。」

===Original===
Early next morning the man brought the horse up to the vineyard. At that time I was not a very good judge of horseflesh. The horse appeared sound and gentle, and, as the owner assured me, had no bad habits. The man wanted a large price for the horse, but finally agreed to accept a much smaller sum, upon payment of which I became possessed of a very fine-looking animal. But alas for the deceitfulness of appearances! I soon ascertained that the horse was blind in one eye, and that the sight of the other was very defective; and not a month elapsed before my purchase developed most of the diseases that horseflesh is heir to, and a more worthless, broken-winded, spavined quadruped never disgraced the noble name of horse. After worrying through two or three months of life, he expired one night in a fit of the colic. I replaced him with a mule, and Julius henceforth had to take his chances of driving some metamorphosed unfortunate.

===Chinese===
第二天一早，那人就把马牵到葡萄园来了。那时候，我相马的眼力还很不成。这匹马看上去健壮温顺，主人又向我担保，它没有任何坏习气。他要价很高，最后总算肯收一个低得多的数目；钱一付，我便成了一匹相貌堂堂的牲口的主人。可是表象欺人，唉！我不久便查明，这匹马一只眼睛是瞎的，另一只眼力也大有问题；不出一个月，我买的这匹马，便把血肉之躯容易承袭的诸般疾病，几乎得了一个遍——这么一头百无一用、喘症缠身、跗节瘤肿的四足兽，从来没有哪一头这样辱没过「马」这个高贵的名号。它苟延残喘地熬了两三个月，一天夜里疝痛发作，就此断了气。我换了头骡子，从此朱利叶斯赶车，只好自求多福，说不定哪天手里挽的缰绳，就套在哪个变了形的苦命人身上。

===Original===
Circumstances that afterwards came to my knowledge created in my mind a strong suspicion that Julius may have played a more than unconscious part in this transaction. Among other significant facts was his appearance, the Sunday following the purchase of the horse, in a new suit of store clothes, which I had seen displayed in the window of Mr. Solomon Cohen’s store on my last visit to town, and had remarked on account of their striking originality of cut and pattern. As I had not recently paid Julius any money, and as he had no property to mortgage, I was driven to conjecture to account for his possession of the means to buy the clothes. Of course I would not charge him with duplicity unless I could prove it, at least to a moral certainty, but for a long time afterwards I took his advice only in small doses and with great discrimination.

===Chinese===
后来我知道的一些情形，在我心里勾起了一团浓重的疑云：这桩买卖里头，朱利叶斯扮演的角色，恐怕不止是无心之失。种种耐人寻味的迹象里有一桩：买马之后的头一个礼拜天，他一身崭新的成衣招摇出场——那身衣裳，我上次进城时在所罗门·科恩（Solomon Cohen）先生的铺子橱窗里见过，还因为它裁剪和花样出奇地新颖别致，多瞧了几眼。我近来没有付过朱利叶斯什么钱，他也没有产业可以典押，他哪来的钱置办这身衣裳，我只好靠猜想圆这个说头。当然，除非至少在情理上确凿无疑，我不会指控他耍两面手法；只是打那以后很长一段时间里，我听他的主意，向来只敢小剂量服用，还要细细甄别。

## Sis’ Becky’s Pickaninny / 贝琪大姐的黑娃娃

===Original===
We had not lived in North Carolina very long before I was able to note a marked improvement in my wife’s health. The ozone-laden air of the surrounding piney woods, the mild and equable climate, the peaceful leisure of country life, had brought about in hopeful measure the cure we had anticipated. Toward the end of our second year, however, her ailment took an unexpected turn for the worse. She became the victim of a settled melancholy, attended with vague forebodings of impending misfortune.

===Chinese===
我们在北卡罗来纳州没住多久，我便看出妻子的健康有了明显起色。四周松林间饱含臭氧的空气、温和匀净的气候、乡居生活安宁的闲适，都以令人欣慰的程度，促成了我们原先指望的痊愈。可是到了第二年年底，她的病却出人意料地转坏了。她被一种积重的忧郁缠住了，心头还压着大祸将至的隐隐预感。

===Original===
“You must keep up her spirits,” said our physician, the best in the neighboring town. “This melancholy lowers her tone too much, tends to lessen her strength, and, if it continue too long, may be fraught with grave consequences.”

===Chinese===
「你们得让她提起精神来，」邻近镇上最好的那位医生说。「这种忧郁太伤她的元气，会渐渐削弱她的体力，要是拖得久了，只怕会有严重的后果。」

===Original===
I tried various expedients to cheer her up. I read novels to her. I had the hands on the place come up in the evening and serenade her with plantation songs. Friends came in sometimes and talked, and frequent letters from the North kept her in touch with her former home. But nothing seemed to rouse her from the depression into which she had fallen.

===Chinese===
我想了种种法子逗她开心。我给她念小说。我让庄园里的工人晚上到近前来，唱种植园的歌谣给她解闷。朋友们有时过来陪她说话，北方频繁的来信也让她和旧日的家保持着联系。可是似乎无论什么，都不能把她从消沉里唤出来。

===Original===
One pleasant afternoon in spring, I placed an armchair in a shaded portion of the front piazza, and filling it with pillows led my wife out of the house and seated her where she would have the pleasantest view of a somewhat monotonous scenery. She was scarcely placed when old Julius came through the yard, and, taking off his tattered straw hat, inquired, somewhat anxiously:﻿—

===Chinese===
春天里一个晴好的下午，我把一把扶手椅安在前头游廊的荫凉处，垫上靠垫，扶妻子出屋坐下，让她对着那片虽说略嫌单调、却也算最耐看的景致。她才坐定，老朱利叶斯就打院子那头走过来，摘下那顶破草帽，略带焦心地问道——

===Original===
“How is you feelin’ dis atternoon, ma’m?”

“She is not very cheerful, Julius,” I said. My wife was apparently without energy enough to speak for herself.

===Chinese===
「您今儿下午觉着怎么样，太太？」

「她不大痛快，朱利叶斯，」我说。我妻子显然连替自己答话的气力都没有。

===Original===
The old man did not seem inclined to go away, so I asked him to sit down. I had noticed, as he came up, that he held some small object in his hand. When he had taken his seat on the top step, he kept fingering this object﻿—what it was I could not quite make out.

===Chinese===
老人一时不像要走的意思，我便请他坐下。他走上来的时候，我留意到他手里攥着个小东西。他在最上一级台阶坐定之后，手里就一直摩挲着那东西——到底是什么，我一时看不明白。

===Original===
“What is that you have there, Julius?” I asked, with mild curiosity.

“Dis is my rabbit foot, suh.”

===Chinese===
「你手里拿的是什么，朱利叶斯？」我怀着淡淡的好奇问道。

「这是俺的兔子脚，先生。」

===Original===
This was at a time before this curious superstition had attained its present jocular popularity among white people, and while I had heard of it before, it had not yet outgrown the charm of novelty.

===Chinese===
那是在这样的年头：这种古怪的迷信，还没有像如今这样在白人中间变成人人打趣的时髦玩意儿。我先前虽也听说过它，它却还没褪尽新鲜的意味。

===Original===
“What do you do with it?”

“I kyars it wid me fer luck, suh.”

===Chinese===
「你拿它做什么用？」

「俺随身带着它，图个吉利，先生。」

===Original===
“Julius,” I observed, half to him and half to my wife, “your people will never rise in the world until they throw off these childish superstitions and learn to live by the light of reason and common sense. How absurd to imagine that the forefoot of a poor dead rabbit, with which he timorously felt his way along through a life surrounded by snares and pitfalls, beset by enemies on every hand, can promote happiness or success, or ward off failure or misfortune!”

===Chinese===
「朱利叶斯，」我说道，一半是对他、一半是对我妻子，「你们这个民族，若不抛开这些孩子气的迷信，学会凭理性和常识的光亮过日子，就永远出不了头。想想吧，一只可怜的死兔子的前脚——它活着的时候，就靠这只前脚，在遍地罗网陷阱、四面埋伏仇敌的一生里战战兢兢地探路——居然能保人幸福发达、消灾挡祸，这有多么荒唐！」

===Original===
“It is ridiculous,” assented my wife, with faint interest.

“Dat’s w’at I tells dese niggers roun’ heah,” said Julius. “De fo’-foot ain’ got no power. It has ter be de hin’-foot, suh﻿—de lef’ hin’-foot er a grabe-ya’d rabbit, killt by a cross-eyed nigger on a da’k night in de full er de moon.”

===Chinese===
「确实荒唐，」我妻子附和道，兴致淡淡的。

「俺跟这一带的黑人们也是这么说的，」朱利叶斯说。「前脚是顶不了事的。得用后脚才灵，先生——还得是坟场里那只兔子的左后脚，还得叫一个斗鸡眼的黑人，赶在月圆时节的一个黑夜里把它打死。」

===Original===
“They must be very rare and valuable,” I said.

“Dey is kinder ska’ce, suh, en dey ain’ no ’mount er money could buy mine, suh. I mought len’ it ter anybody I sot sto’ by, but I wouldn’ sell it, no indeed, suh, I wouldn’.”

===Chinese===
「那想必非常稀罕、非常值钱了，」我说。

「是有些难得，先生，俺这一只啊，出多大的价钱也买不走。俺瞧得上的人，兴许还能借给他使使，可俺绝不卖，真不卖，先生，俺不卖。」

===Original===
“How do you know it brings good luck?” I asked.

“ ’Ca’se I ain’ had no bad luck sence I had it, suh, en I’s had dis rabbit foot fer fo’ty yeahs. I had a good marster befo’ de wah, en I wa’n’t sol’ erway, en I wuz sot free; en dat ’uz all good luck.”

===Chinese===
「你怎么知道它带得来好运？」我问。

「因为打俺有了它，就没遭过一回霉运，先生，俺这兔子脚带上身都有四十年了。南北战争还没打起来的时候，俺有位好主人；俺没叫人卖到别处去，后来还得了自由——这全是好运哪。」

===Original===
“But that doesn’t prove anything,” I rejoined. “Many other people have gone through a similar experience, and probably more than one of them had no rabbit’s foot.”

“Law, suh! you doan hafter prove ’bout de rabbit foot! Eve’ybody knows dat; leas’ways eve’ybody roun’ heah knows it. But ef it has ter be prove’ ter folks w’at wa’n’t bawn en raise’ in dis naberhood, dey is a’ easy way ter prove it. Is I eber tol’ you de tale er Sis’ Becky en her pickaninny?”

===Chinese===
「可这证明不了什么，」我接口道。「经历过同样事情的人多了，他们里头保不齐不止一个压根儿就没有兔子脚。」

「哎呀，先生！兔子脚的事哪儿用得着证明！这是人人皆知的；至少这一带的人全知道。可要是非得证明给那些不是在这地界里生、这地界里长的人听，倒有个省事的法子。俺可曾跟您讲过贝琪大姐（Sis' Becky）和她那黑娃娃（pickaninny）的故事？」

===Original===
“No,” I said, “let us hear it.” I thought perhaps the story might interest my wife as much or more than the novel I had meant to read from.

===Chinese===
「没讲过，」我说，「讲来听听吧。」我心想，这故事或许能像我原先打算念的那本小说一样叫我妻子感兴趣，说不定还更胜一筹。

===Original===
“Dis yer Becky,” Julius began, “useter b’long ter ole Kunnel Pen’leton, who owned a plantation down on de Wim’l’ton Road, ’bout ten miles fum heah, des befo’ you gits ter Black Swamp. Dis yer Becky wuz a fiel’-han’, en a monst’us good ’un. She had a husban’ oncet, a nigger w’at b’longed on de nex’ plantation, but de man w’at owned her husban’ died, en his lan’ en his niggers had ter be sol’ fer ter pay his debts. Kunnel Pen’leton ’lowed he’d ’a’ bought dis nigger, but he had be’n bettin’ on hoss races, en didn’ hab no money, en so Becky’s husban’ wuz sol’ erway ter Fuhginny.

===Chinese===
「这个贝琪呀，」朱利叶斯开了头，「从前是老彭伯顿上校的人。上校在威明顿大道边上有座庄园，离这儿十来英里，就在快到黑沼泽（Black Swamp）的地方。这个贝琪是个田里的好劳力，顶顶好的一个。她从前有过一个男人，是隔壁庄园上的黑人；可她男人那位主人死了，地也好、黑人也好，都得卖掉抵债。彭伯顿上校说他原是要把这个黑人买下来的，无奈他一直在赛马上押注，手头一个钱也没有，结果贝琪的男人就被卖到弗吉尼亚（Fuhginny）去了。

===Original===
“Co’se Becky went on some ’bout losin’ her man, but she couldn’ he’p herse’f; en ’sides dat, she had her pickaninny fer ter comfo’t her. Dis yer little Mose wuz de cutes’, blackes’, shiny-eyedes’ little nigger you eber laid eyes on, en he wuz ez fon’ er his mammy ez his mammy wuz er him. Co’se Becky had ter wuk en didn’ hab much time ter was’e wid her baby. Ole Aun’ Nancy, de plantation nuss down at de qua’ters, useter take keer er little Mose in de daytime, en atter de niggers come in fum de cotton-fiel’ Becky ’ud git her chile en kiss ’im en nuss ’im, en keep ’im ’tel mawnin’; en on Sundays she’d hab ’im in her cabin wid her all day long.

===Chinese===
「丢了男人，贝琪自然是又哭又闹了一场，可她做不得自己的主；再说，她还有黑娃娃给她宽心呢。这个小摩西（Mose）啊，是你这辈子见也没见过的最乖巧、最黑亮、眼睛最亮的小黑人娃儿，他黏他娘，跟他娘疼他一分不差。当然，贝琪得下地干活，没有多少工夫耗在娃娃身上。住在黑奴住区那头的庄园嬷嬷南希大娘（Aun' Nancy），白天就照看着小摩西；等黑人从棉花地里收工回来，贝琪就把孩子接过去，亲他、哺他，一直搂到天亮；到了礼拜天，她就让孩子整天待在自己小屋里，寸步不离。

===Original===
“Sis’ Becky had got sorter useter gittin’ ’long widout her husban’, w’en one day Kunnel Pen’leton went ter de races. Co’se w’en he went ter de races, he tuk his hosses, en co’se he bet on ’is own hosses, en co’se he los’ his money; fer Kunnel Pen’leton didn’ nebber hab no luck wid his hosses, ef he did keep hisse’f po’ projeckin’ wid ’em. But dis time dey wuz a hoss name’ Lightnin’ Bug, w’at b’longed ter ernudder man, en dis hoss won de sweep-stakes; en Kunnel Pen’leton tuk a lackin’ ter dat hoss, en ax’ his owner w’at he wuz willin’ ter take fer ’im.

===Chinese===
「贝琪大姐没了男人，渐渐也过得惯了。偏偏有一天，彭伯顿上校跑赛马会去了。上校去赛马会，自然要带上自家的马；自然要在自家的马上下注；也自然是输了钱——因为彭伯顿上校养马从来没交过一回好运，净顾着捣鼓马，把自己都捣鼓穷了。可这一回，有一匹名叫「闪电虫」（Lightnin' Bug）的马，是别人家的，偏偏这匹马赢走了全场头彩。彭伯顿上校一眼相中了这匹马，便问马主人肯要个什么价。

===Original===
“ ‘I’ll take a thousan’ dollahs fer dat hoss,’ sez dis yer man, who had a big plantation down to’ds Wim’l’ton, whar he raise’ hosses fer ter race en ter sell.

“Well, Kunnel Pen’leton scratch’ ’is head, en wonder whar he wuz gwine ter raise a thousan’ dollahs; en he didn’ see des how he could do it, fer he owed ez much ez he could borry a’ready on de skyo’ity he could gib. But he wuz des boun’ ter hab dat hoss, so sezee:﻿—

===Chinese===
「『这匹马我要价一千块，』这人说。他在威明顿那头有座大庄园，专养马参赛、出卖。
